a mere figurehead = 纯粹挂名的首脑

figurine
核心：小雕像、小塑像：摆在架子上的那种瓷或树脂小人。可数。
① A figurine of a shepherdess survived four house moves and one flood.
= 一尊牧羊女小瓷像，扛过四次搬家和一次水灾。
a figurine = 小雕像
② The shelf holds nine figurines and a dust cloth that never gets used.
= 那个架子上摆着九尊小塑像，还有一块从没用过的抹布。
a china figurine = 瓷制小塑像

Fijian
核心：斐济的、斐济人，也指斐济语：形容词与名词同形。
① The Fijian team sings before the match and again after it.
= 斐济队赛前唱歌，赛后再唱一次。
the Fijian team = 斐济队
② Two Fijians in that squad grew up within a mile of each other.
= 那支队里的两个斐济人，是在相距不到一英里的地方长大的。
a Fijian = 斐济人

filament
核心：两支。一是（灯泡的）灯丝；二是（生物、材料）细丝、丝状体。可数。
① The filament goes the moment you switch the lamp on, never while it burns.
= 灯丝总在开灯那一下断，从不在亮着的时候断。
a filament = 灯丝
② Each filament is thinner than a hair and carries the whole current.
= 每一根细丝比头发还细，却承着全部电流。
a fine filament = 极细的丝

filbert
核心：榛子：一种榛果，跟 hazelnut 基本是一回事，果壳更长些。可数。
① Filberts fall in September and the squirrels get a fortnight's start.
= 榛子九月落下，松鼠比人早开工两个星期。
a filbert = 榛子
② A pound of filberts makes two jars of something nobody can stop eating.
= 一磅榛子能做出两罐让人停不下嘴的东西。
filberts = 榛子（复数）

filch
核心：（非正式）顺手拿走：偷不值钱的小东西，语气比 steal 轻得多。
① Somebody filched the good scissors and left the blunt pair behind.
= 有人顺走了那把好剪刀，把钝的那把留下了。
filch something = 顺走某物
② Biscuits get filched from that tin faster than anybody replaces them.
= 那个铁罐里的饼干，被顺走的速度比补进去的还快。
be filched = 被顺走

file
核心：五支。一是文件夹、卷宗：a personnel file；二是（电脑）文件：save a file；
三是动词，归档、正式提交：file a report、file for divorce；
四是锉刀，也作动词锉；五是一列纵队：in single file。习语 on file 是存档备查。
① The personnel file has three sheets and a photograph nobody recognises.
= 那份人事档案有三页纸，还有一张谁也认不出的照片。
a personnel file = 人事档案
on file = 存档备查的
② Save the file before you answer the phone, not after it.
= 接电话之前存文件，别接完了再存。
save a file = 保存文件
③ She filed for divorce on a Tuesday and told nobody until the Friday.
= 她周二递了离婚申请，到周五才跟人说。
file for divorce = 申请离婚
file a report = 提交报告
④ He filed the key down until it turned and then had two copies cut.
= 他把钥匙锉到能转动，然后配了两把。
file something down = 把某物锉小
⑤ The children came in in single file and left in every direction at once.
= 孩子们排成一列进来，散开时各奔东西。
in single file = 排成一列纵队
⑥ The file clerk keeps a file cabinet that predates file sharing by thirty years.
= 那位档案员管着一个比文件共享早三十年的文件柜。
a file cabinet = 文件柜（美式）
a file clerk = 档案员
file sharing = 文件共享

filename
核心：（电脑）文件名：后面多带扩展名。可数。
① The filename has a date in it and the date is wrong.
= 那个文件名里带着日期，日期是错的。
a filename = 文件名
② A filename with a space in it breaks that script every single time.
= 文件名里带空格，那个脚本每回都崩。
change the filename = 改文件名

filet
核心：（美式拼法）去骨的肉片、鱼片：英式写 fillet；
最常见的说法是 filet mignon。
① The filet mignon is the smallest cut and the biggest number on the menu.
= 菲力牛排是最小的一块肉，菜单上却是最大的那个数字。
filet mignon = 菲力牛排
② A filet of sole cooks in the time it takes to warm the plate.
= 一片去骨鳎鱼，盘子热好的工夫就熟了。
a filet = 去骨的肉片或鱼片

filial
核心：（正式）子女的、孝顺的：filial duty、filial piety。
① Filial duty in that family means a phone call every Sunday at six.
= 那家人的孝道，就是每周日六点打个电话。
filial duty = 子女的本分
② Filial piety and a train ticket do not always arrive together.
= 孝心跟一张火车票，未必总是同时到位。
filial piety = 孝道

filibuster
核心：（政治）冗长发言阻挠议事：靠一直讲话把议案拖死。名词与动词同形。
① The filibuster lasted eleven hours and the bill passed the following week.
= 那场冗长阻挠讲了十一个钟头，法案下一周照样通过。
a filibuster = 冗长发言阻挠
② He filibustered until the clock ran out and drank nine glasses of water.
= 他一直讲到散会时间，喝了九杯水。
filibuster a bill = 用冗长发言拖死一项议案

filigree
核心：金银丝细工：把细丝盘成花纹的装饰；引申指精细繁复的纹样。不可数。
① The filigree on the lid is silver and the box underneath it is tin.
= 盒盖上的细丝花是银的，底下的盒子是马口铁。
filigree = 金银丝细工
② A filigree of frost covered the window and lasted until nine.
= 一层花纹般的霜盖住窗子，一直留到九点。
a filigree of something = 某物结成的细密花纹

filing
核心：两支。一是归档这件活儿：do the filing；二是复数 filings，锉屑。
① The filing takes an afternoon a week and gets left for a month.
= 归档一星期要花一个下午，往往一拖就是一个月。
do the filing = 做归档
a filing cabinet = 文件柜
② Iron filings on a sheet of paper show the field better than any diagram.
= 纸上的铁屑，把磁场显示得比任何示意图都清楚。
iron filings = 铁屑
③ The filing clerk left in June and the filing itself did not.
= 那位归档员六月走了，归档的活儿留了下来。
a filing clerk = 归档员

Filipina
核心：菲律宾女子：只说女性；男性与统称用 Filipino。可数。
① A Filipina nurse trained here in 1998 and runs the ward now.
= 一位菲律宾护士 1998 年在这里受训，如今管着整个病房。
a Filipina = 菲律宾女子
② Two Filipinas share a flat and send most of what they earn home.
= 两位菲律宾女子合租一套房，挣的大半寄回了家。

Filipino
核心：菲律宾的、菲律宾人；也指菲律宾语。女性单独称 Filipina。
① Filipino families in that street cook for everybody at Christmas.
= 那条街上的菲律宾家庭，圣诞节给所有人做饭。
a Filipino = 菲律宾人
② Filipino is taught in two schools in the city and spoken in forty.
= 菲律宾语在城里两所学校教，在四十所学校有人说。
Filipino = 菲律宾语

fill
核心：四支。一是装满、填满：fill a glass；二是填补空缺：fill a vacancy；
三是名词，足量：eat your fill；四是短语动词 fill in 与 fill up。
习语 fill the bill 是「正合适」。
① Fill the glass to the line and not to the top.
= 倒到那条线为止，别倒满。
fill a glass = 把杯子倒满
② The vacancy was filled by somebody who had left the year before.
= 那个空缺，由一位前一年离职的人补上了。
fill a vacancy = 填补空缺
③ They ate their fill and took the rest home in a box.
= 他们吃了个饱，剩下的装盒带回家。
eat your fill = 吃个饱
④ Fill in the form in black ink and fill up the car before you set off.
= 表格用黑墨水填，出发前把车加满油。
fill in something = 填写某物
fill up = 加满
⑤ A folding table fills the bill and costs eleven pounds.
= 一张折叠桌正合适，才十一镑钱。
fill the bill = 正合适
⑥ Fill out the form in block capitals and post it before Thursday.
= 表格用正楷填，周四之前寄出去。
fill out = 填写（美式）
⑦ They fill up on bread and leave the expensive part on the plate.
= 他们拿面包填饱肚子，贵的那部分留在盘子里。
fill up on something = 拿某物填饱肚子
⑧ A fill light softens the shadow that the main lamp makes.
= 补光灯柔化主灯打出的那道阴影。
a fill light = 补光灯

filler
核心：三支。一是填料、腻子：wood filler；二是（版面、时间的）补白：a filler item；
三是说话时的填充词，如 um 与 you know。可数。
① Wood filler dries lighter than the wood and everybody can see the repair.
= 木用腻子干了之后比木头浅，修过的地方谁都看得出。
wood filler = 木用腻子
② The bulletin ran a filler about a duck and dropped the council story.
= 那档新闻插了一条关于鸭子的补白，把议会那条撤了。
a filler item = 补白的小条目
③ The filler cap is on the wrong side at every petrol station in the county.
= 全郡的加油站，油箱盖都在不对的那一边。
a filler cap = 油箱盖

fillet
核心：（英式）去骨的肉片、鱼片；也作动词，给鱼或肉剔骨。美式拼 filet。
① The fishmonger fillets it in nine seconds and wraps it in two.
= 鱼贩九秒钟剔完骨，两秒钟包好。
fillet a fish = 给鱼剔骨
② A fillet of beef feeds four and costs what eight would cost elsewhere.
= 一块牛里脊够四个人吃，价钱抵别处八个人的。
a fillet of beef = 牛里脊

filling
核心：三支。一是（牙齿的）补料；二是（食物的）馅；三是形容词，扛饿的。
① The filling came out on a toffee and the dentist was closed until Monday.
= 补牙的填料被一块太妃糖粘掉了，牙医周一才开门。
a filling = 补牙的填料
② The filling is mostly onion and the pastry is mostly air.
= 那馅儿多半是洋葱，那酥皮多半是空气。
the filling = 馅料
③ Porridge is filling, cheap and taken very seriously in that house.
= 燕麦粥扛饿、便宜，在那家人眼里是很正经的东西。
a filling meal = 扛饿的一顿饭
④ The filling station on that road closed and the next one is nineteen miles away.
= 那条路上的加油站关了，下一个在十九英里外。
a filling station = 加油站

fillip
核心：（正式）提振、一剂强心针：给事情添把劲的东西。可数，
最常见的说法是 give something a fillip。
① The grant gave the whole scheme a fillip and paid for one van.
= 那笔拨款给整个项目提了口气，买下一辆货车。
give something a fillip = 给某事添把劲
② A dry week in June was exactly the fillip the harvest needed.
= 六月里干爽的一个星期，正是收成需要的那口气。
a fillip = 一剂提振

filly
核心：小母马：四岁以下的雌马，跟小公马 colt 相对。可数。
① The filly ran second and cost more than the horse that won.
= 那匹小母马跑了第二，身价却比冠军还高。
a filly = 小母马
② Two fillies were entered and only one of them had ever seen a crowd.
= 有两匹小母马报了名，只有一匹见过人山人海。

film
核心：四支。一是电影：a film star、see a film；二是胶卷：a roll of film；
三是薄膜、一层：a film of dust；四是动词，拍摄。
① A film star opened the shop and the queue went round two corners.
= 一位电影明星给那家店剪彩，队伍拐了两个弯。
a film star = 电影明星
see a film = 看电影
② A roll of film sat in a drawer for eleven years and came out fine.
= 一卷胶卷在抽屉里躺了十一年，冲出来居然没坏。
a roll of film = 一卷胶卷
③ A film of dust on the piano says how long the family has been away.
= 钢琴上那层薄灰，说明那家人走了多久。
a film of dust = 一层薄灰
④ They filmed the whole street in a day and used forty seconds of it.
= 他们一天拍完整条街，用上的只有四十秒。
film something = 拍摄某物
⑤ The film-maker spent a year on film-making and eleven minutes on the title.
= 那位电影人拍片花了一年，起片名花了十一分钟。
a film-maker = 电影人
film-making = 电影制作
⑥ The film certificate says fifteen and the queue is mostly twelve.
= 那部片子定级十五岁，排队的多半十二岁。
a film certificate = 电影分级
⑦ Film noir is mostly rain, hats and light coming through blinds.
= 黑色电影多半就是雨、帽子，还有百叶窗缝里透进来的光。
film noir = 黑色电影

filmgoer
核心：常看电影的人、影迷。可数。
① Filmgoers in that town have one screen between them and very firm opinions.
= 那个镇上的影迷共用一块银幕，看法却极坚定。
a filmgoer = 常看电影的人
② The average filmgoer arrives after the trailers and leaves before the credits.
= 一般影迷预告片放完才进场，字幕没完就走。

filmic
核心：（正式）电影的、有电影感的：说手法与画面像电影，而不像戏剧或小说。
① The novel is filmic and three studios have optioned it and done nothing.
= 那部小说很有电影感，三家片厂买了改编权，都没动。
filmic = 有电影感的
② A filmic sense of pace is what makes the middle chapters move.
= 正是一种电影式的节奏感，让中间几章推得动。
filmic techniques = 电影化的手法

filming
核心：拍摄这件事：不可数 —— filming starts、filming schedule。
① Filming starts in April and the street will be closed for six weeks.
= 四月开拍，那条街要封六个星期。
filming = 拍摄
② Filming in that weather cost them nine days and one camera.
= 在那种天气里拍摄，让他们赔进九天和一台摄影机。
a filming schedule = 拍摄日程

filmography
核心：（影视）作品年表：某位导演或演员拍过的全部片子的清单。可数。
① Her filmography runs to sixty titles and four that nobody can find.
= 她的作品年表有六十部，其中四部谁也找不到。
a filmography = 作品年表
② The filmography at the back is more use than the essay in front of it.
= 书后那份作品年表，比它前面那篇文章有用。
compile a filmography = 编一份作品年表

filmstrip
核心：（旧）教学幻灯胶片：一长条连着的静态画面，课堂上一格一格地放。可数。
① The filmstrip advanced with a click and half the class waited for the next one.
= 幻灯胶片咔一声换一格，半个班都在等下一格。
a filmstrip = 教学幻灯胶片
② A filmstrip and a tape recorder counted as technology in 1974.
= 1974 年，一条幻灯胶片加一台录音机就算高科技了。

filmy
核心：薄如蝉翼的、轻薄透光的：说布料，也说雾气与云。
① A filmy curtain keeps the sun out and nothing else at all.
= 一层轻薄的窗帘挡得住太阳，别的什么都挡不住。
a filmy curtain = 轻薄的窗帘
② Filmy cloud came over the moon and the frost held off till dawn.
= 一层薄云遮住月亮，霜一直到天亮才下。
filmy cloud = 薄云

filter
核心：三支。一是滤器、滤芯：a coffee filter；二是动词，过滤：filter water；
三是（信息、消息）筛除与渗透：filter out、filter through。
① The coffee filter is paper and that machine takes a different size.
= 那种咖啡滤纸是纸的，那台机器要的却是另一个尺寸。
a coffee filter = 咖啡滤纸
② They filter the water twice and still boil it before drinking.
= 他们把水过滤两遍，喝之前还是要煮开。
filter water = 过滤水
③ The system filters out anything with two exclamation marks in it.
= 那套系统会把带两个感叹号的东西全筛掉。
filter out = 筛除
④ News of the closure filtered through the building by lunchtime.
= 关门的消息到午饭时已经在整栋楼里传开。
filter through = 渐渐传开
⑤ A filter tip takes out some of it and none of the habit.
= 过滤嘴滤掉一部分，习惯一点没滤掉。
a filter tip = 过滤嘴

filth
核心：两支。一是污秽、脏东西：be covered in filth；二是（贬）下流的言语与图像。
不可数。形容词 filthy。
① The cellar was covered in filth that turned out to be nine years of soot.
= 地窖里满是污垢，原来是积了九年的烟灰。
be covered in filth = 满是污垢
② The letter is filth and the newspaper printed only the polite half.
= 那封信满纸污言秽语，报纸只登了客气的那一半。
filth = 下流的东西

filthiness
核心：肮脏、污秽：不可数；也说言语的下流。
① The filthiness of the kitchen was the only thing the inspector wrote down.
= 厨房之脏，是检查员唯一记下来的事。
the filthiness of something = 某处的肮脏
② Filthiness in a hospital corridor is a management failure, not a cleaning one.
= 医院走廊脏，是管理的失败，不是保洁的失败。
filthiness = 肮脏

filthy
核心：三支。一是极脏的：filthy hands；二是下流的：a filthy joke；
三是（英式口语）糟透的：filthy weather；另有 filthy rich，富得流油。副词 filthily。
① He came in with filthy hands and washed them in the wrong sink.
= 他手脏兮兮地进来，在不该用的那个水槽里洗了。
filthy hands = 脏手
② A filthy joke at that table cost him the contract.
= 在那张桌上讲了个下流笑话，他把合同弄丢了。
a filthy joke = 下流笑话
③ Filthy weather kept the boats in the harbour and the pub full.
= 恶劣的天气把船留在港里，把酒馆挤满。
filthy weather = 糟透的天气
filthy rich = 富得流油
副词 filthily：脏兮兮地、下流地，用得不多。
④ The yard was filthily neglected and the garden behind it was immaculate.
= 那个院子脏得没人打理，后头的花园却一尘不染。
filthily neglected = 脏得无人搭理

filtrate
核心：（技术）滤液：过滤之后流出来的那部分液体。可数不可数都行。
① The filtrate is clear and the residue is where the interesting part sits.
= 滤液是清的，有意思的东西都在滤渣里。
a filtrate = 滤液
② They keep the filtrate for testing and throw the rest of it away.
= 他们留下滤液做检测，其余的都倒掉。
collect the filtrate = 收集滤液

filtration
核心：过滤这个过程：不可数 —— a filtration system、water filtration。
① The filtration system runs all night and the pool is clear by six.
= 那套过滤系统整夜运转，六点池水就清了。
a filtration system = 过滤系统
② Water filtration removes the taste and the argument about it continues.
= 水过滤把那股怪味去掉了，关于它的争论还在继续。
water filtration = 水过滤

fin
核心：三支。一是鱼鳍；二是（飞机、火箭的）尾翼；三是潜水用的脚蹼。可数。
① A fin broke the surface twice and the beach emptied within a minute.
= 一只鳍两次划开水面，海滩一分钟就空了。
a fin = 鱼鳍
② The tail fin is the only part of that aircraft they repainted.
= 尾翼是那架飞机上唯一重新喷过漆的部分。
a tail fin = 尾翼
③ The building is fin de siècle and the plumbing in it is older still.
= 那栋楼是世纪末风格，里头的管道比它还老。
fin de siècle = 世纪末（风格）

finagle
核心：（非正式）耍手腕弄到手、连蒙带混地办成：
finagle something out of somebody。
① He finagled two tickets out of a man who had none to spare.
= 他从一个根本没有余票的人手里，硬是弄到了两张。
finagle something = 耍手腕弄到某物
② She finagled her way onto the list and stayed on it for nine years.
= 她连蒙带混挤进了名单，一待就是九年。
finagle your way into something = 想方设法挤进某处

final
核心：三支。一是最后的：the final chapter；二是（决定）不可更改的；
三是名词，决赛：the cup final；复数 finals 指期末大考。
① The final chapter answers a question nobody asked in the first eleven.
= 最后一章回答的那个问题，前十一章里没人问过。
the final chapter = 最后一章
② The decision is final and the letter says so in bold type.
= 那个决定不可更改，信上用黑体写着。
the decision is final = 决定不可更改
③ The cup final was decided by a goal that took four minutes to allow.
= 那场杯赛决赛，是被一个判了四分钟才算数的进球定下来的。
the cup final = 杯赛决赛
finals = 期末大考
④ A final clause answers the question why and usually starts with so that.
= 目的状语从句回答「为什么」，通常以「so that」开头。
a final clause = 目的状语从句

finale
核心：（演出、乐曲的）终场、终曲：最后那一段最热闹的部分。可数。
① The finale needs nine people on stage and eleven were available.
= 终场要九个人上台，能到场的有十一个。
the finale = 终场、终曲
② A grand finale with fireworks emptied the car park before the encore.
= 一场带烟火的盛大终场，返场之前就把停车场清空了。
a grand finale = 盛大的终场

finalist
核心：进入决赛的选手或队伍。可数。
① Every finalist gets a certificate and exactly one of them gets the cheque.
= 每位决赛选手都有证书，支票只有一个人拿。
a finalist = 决赛选手
② Two finalists trained in the same pool and never met until that day.
= 两位决赛选手在同一个泳池训练，到那天才头一回见面。

finality
核心：（正式）终局性、不容更改：说语气或裁定摆明了没得商量。不可数。
① There was a finality in her tone that ended the discussion on the spot.
= 她那语气里有种不容置疑的意味，讨论当场就结束了。
a finality = 不容更改的意味
② The finality of the ruling surprised even the side that had won.
= 那项裁定的终局性，连赢的一方都吃了一惊。
the finality of something = 某事的终局性

finalization
核心：（正式）定案、最后敲定：英式也拼 finalisation。不可数。
① Finalization of the accounts waits on one signature and one holiday.
= 账目定案，就等一个签名和一个人休完假。
finalization = 定案
② The finalization of the contract took three weeks longer than the negotiation.
= 合同的最后敲定，比谈判本身多花了三个星期。
the finalization of something = 某事的最后敲定

finalize
核心：把……定下来、敲定：英式也拼 finalise，多说 finalize the details。
① Finalize the seating plan before the invitations go out, not after.
= 请柬发出去之前把座位表定下来，别等发完再定。
finalize the details = 敲定细节
② The route was finalized in March and then changed twice in April.
= 那条路线三月定了案，四月又改了两回。
be finalized = 被最终定下

finally
核心：三支。一是终于、总算：等了很久之后，带一点松口气的意味；
二是列举时的「最后一点」，跟 firstly、secondly 成套；三是一锤定音地、最终地。
① The parcel finally arrived on a day when nobody was in.
= 那个包裹总算到了，偏偏那天家里没人。
finally = 终于、总算
② Finally, and briefly, the roof still leaks.
= 最后一点，简单说：屋顶还在漏。
finally = 最后（列举用语）
③ The matter was finally settled by a letter that nobody kept.
= 那件事最终是靠一封没人留存的信了结的。
be finally settled = 最终了结

finance
核心：三支。一是财政、金融：public finance，不可数；二是资金：raise finance；
三是动词，出资：finance a project。复数 finances 指某人的财务状况。
① Public finance is dull until the year the money runs out.
= 公共财政很枯燥，直到钱花光的那一年。
public finance = 公共财政
personal finance = 个人理财
② They raised finance from three banks and spent it on one building.
= 他们从三家银行筹到资金，全花在了一栋楼上。
raise finance = 筹措资金
③ Her finances recovered a good deal faster than her patience did.
= 她的财务状况恢复得比她的耐心快得多。
somebody's finances = 某人的财务状况
④ The finance company lends at a rate the shop never mentions out loud.
= 那家财务公司放贷的利率，店里从来不明说。
a finance company = 财务公司

financial
核心：财政的、金融的、财务的：the financial year、financial difficulties。
副词 financially。
① The financial year ends in March and the panic starts in February.
= 财政年度三月结束，恐慌二月就开始了。
the financial year = 财政年度
② Financial difficulties closed the shop and the landlord kept the sign.
= 财务困难让那家店关了门，招牌被房东留下了。
financial difficulties = 财务困难
副词 financially：在财务上、经济上，多说 financially viable、financially independent。
③ The scheme is financially viable and politically impossible.
= 那个方案财务上可行，政治上没门。
financially viable = 财务上可行的
④ She has been financially independent since she was nineteen.
= 她从十九岁起就在经济上独立了。
financially independent = 经济独立的
⑤ Financial aid covers the fees and does not cover the bus fare.
= 助学金能覆盖学费，覆盖不了车费。
financial aid = 助学金、财政援助

financials
核心：（商业）财务报表：一家公司那套账目数字，作复数。
① The financials look tidy and the footnotes are where the year really is.
= 那套财务报表看着规整，这一年的实情都在脚注里。
the financials = 财务报表
② Nobody reads the financials until somebody wants to buy the company.
= 财务报表没人看，直到有人想买下这家公司。
publish the financials = 公布财务报表

financier
核心：（正式）金融家、出资人：靠资本运作为业的人。可数。
① A financier bought the pier and left it exactly as it was.
= 一位金融家买下那座栈桥，原样放着没动。
a financier = 金融家
② Two financiers backed the scheme and only one of them read it first.
= 两位出资人支持那个方案，事先读过的只有一位。

financing
核心：融资、资金安排：不可数 —— arrange financing。
① Financing for the bridge took longer to arrange than the bridge took to build.
= 那座桥的融资谈下来，比造桥还费时间。
arrange financing = 安排融资
② The financing falls apart if any one bank changes its mind.
= 只要有一家银行改主意，那笔融资就散了。
financing = 融资

finch
核心：雀类小鸟：喙短而厚、专吃种子的一类小鸟，金翅雀就是其中一种。可数。
① A finch takes the sunflower seeds and leaves the rest of the mix.
= 那只雀专挑葵花籽吃，混合料里别的一概不碰。
a finch = 雀类小鸟
② Finches turn up in twos and quarrel over a single perch.
= 雀鸟总是成对地来，为一根站杆吵个不停。

find
核心：五支。一是找到、发现：find a seat；二是发觉、觉得：find it hard to do something；
三是（法律）裁定：find somebody guilty；四是名词，发现物：a lucky find；
五是短语动词 find out。习语 find your feet 是适应下来。
过去式与过去分词都是 found。
① Find a seat near the door and you can leave when the speeches start.
= 找个靠门的座位，一开始致辞你就能溜。
find a seat = 找个座位
② She finds it hard to say no and very easy to look busy.
= 她很难说出一个「不」字，装忙倒容易得很。
find it hard to do something = 觉得做某事很难
③ The jury found him guilty on one count and disagreed on the rest.
= 陪审团裁定他一项罪名成立，其余的没能一致。
find somebody guilty = 裁定某人有罪
④ The bowl was a lucky find in a box of broken things.
= 那只碗是在一箱破烂里捡到的宝。
a lucky find = 捡到的宝
⑤ It took her a term to find her feet and a year to find out why.
= 她用一个学期适应下来，用一年弄明白为什么。
find your feet = 适应下来
find out = 弄清楚
⑥ The court found for the tenant and found against the agency twice.
= 法庭判租客胜诉，两次判中介败诉。
find for somebody = 判某人胜诉
find against somebody = 判某人败诉

finder
核心：拾得者、找到东西的人；也指望远镜上的寻星镜。可数。
习语 finders keepers 是「谁捡到归谁」，多是孩子话。
① The finder handed the wallet in and left no name at the desk.
= 捡到钱包的人交了上去，在前台没留名字。
a finder = 拾得者
② Finders keepers works in a playground and nowhere at all in law.
= 「谁捡到归谁」在操场上说得通，在法律里哪儿都说不通。
finders keepers = 谁捡到归谁

finding
核心：两支。一是（调查、研究的）结论，多用复数 findings；
二是（法律）裁定：a finding of negligence。可数。
① The findings fill nine pages and the summary contradicts two of them.
= 那些结论写了九页，摘要跟其中两条自相矛盾。
the findings = 调查结论
publish the findings = 公布结论
② A finding of negligence changed what the insurer was willing to pay.
= 一项过失认定，改变了保险公司愿意赔的数目。
a finding of negligence = 过失认定

fine
核心：五支。一是好的、不错的：a fine day；二是细的、精细的：fine sand；
三是罚款（名词与动词）：pay a fine；四是（勉强）还行；
五是习语：cut it fine 是时间掐得太紧，a fine line 是一线之差。副词 finely。
① It turned into a fine day and the forecast never admitted it.
= 后来天气很好，天气预报始终没承认。
a fine day = 好天气
② Fine sand gets into the camera and the camera never quite recovers.
= 细沙钻进相机，相机就再没彻底好过。
fine sand = 细沙
③ The fine was forty pounds and the parking would have cost two.
= 罚款四十镑，本来停车费两镑。
pay a fine = 交罚款
④ They cut it fine and made the ferry with ninety seconds to spare.
= 他们时间掐得太紧，赶上渡轮时只剩九十秒。
cut it fine = 时间掐得太紧
⑤ There is a fine line between confident and unbearable.
= 自信与讨人厌之间，只有一线之差。
a fine line = 一线之差
副词 finely：一是细细地：finely chopped；二是精确地：finely balanced。
⑥ The onion needs to be finely chopped and the garlic does not.
= 洋葱要切得极细，蒜就不必。
finely chopped = 切得极细的
⑦ The argument is finely balanced and one letter tipped it over.
= 那场争论势均力敌，一封信就把它压偏了。
finely balanced = 势均力敌的
⑧ They went through the accounts with a fine-tooth comb and fine-tuned nothing.
= 他们拿细齿梳把账目梳了一遍，一处也没微调。
a fine-tooth comb = 细齿梳（细查）
fine-tune = 微调
fine-tuning = 微调（名词）

fineness
核心：两支。一是精细、细密；二是（金银的）成色、纯度。不可数。
① The fineness of the mesh decides what gets through and what stays.
= 网眼的细密程度，决定什么过得去、什么留下。
the fineness of something = 某物的细密
② Fineness is stamped on the inside of the ring in three tiny numbers.
= 纯度用三个小数字打在戒指内圈上。
fineness = 成色、纯度

finery
核心：华服、盛装：郑重场合穿戴的漂亮衣饰。不可数。
① The whole family turned up in finery and stood in the rain for an hour.
= 全家人盛装到场，在雨里站了一个钟头。
finery = 盛装
② Her finery came out of a suitcase that lives on top of the wardrobe.
= 她那身华服，是从衣柜顶上那只箱子里翻出来的。
in all your finery = 盛装打扮地

fines herbes
核心：（法餐）细香草碎：欧芹、细香葱、龙蒿与山萝卜切碎的混合香草。作复数。
① Fines herbes go in at the very end and lose everything if they boil.
= 细香草最后才下锅，一煮就什么味道都没了。
fines herbes = 法式细香草碎
② An omelette with fines herbes is the test that kitchen sets its cooks.
= 一份细香草蛋卷，是那间厨房考厨子的题。
an omelette with fines herbes = 细香草蛋卷

finesse
核心：两支。一是（正式）手法的精妙圆熟：handle something with finesse；
二是动词，巧妙地绕开或处理。不可数。
① She handled the complaint with a finesse that the letter did not deserve.
= 她处理那桩投诉的手法之圆熟，那封信其实不配。
with finesse = 手法圆熟地
② He finessed the question and answered a different one rather well.
= 他把那个问题巧妙绕开，另答了一个，答得还挺好。
finesse something = 巧妙地绕开某事

finger
核心：三支加一串习语。一是手指；二是条状的食物：fish fingers；三是动词，用手指摸。
习语：keep your fingers crossed 是祈求好运，put your finger on 是说准症结，
point the finger at 是把矛头指向某人。
① She broke a finger in a door and typed with nine of them for a month.
= 她的手指被门夹断，用剩下九根打了一个月的字。
a finger = 手指
② Keep your fingers crossed until the second envelope arrives.
= 第二个信封到之前，先祈祷着。
keep your fingers crossed = 祈求好运
cross one's fingers = 交叉手指祈求好运
③ Nobody could put a finger on what had changed about that room.
= 那间屋子究竟哪儿变了，谁也说不上来。
put your finger on something = 说准某事的症结
④ The report points the finger at a department that no longer exists.
= 那份报告把矛头指向一个已经不存在的部门。
point the finger at somebody = 指认某人
⑤ Fish fingers and peas is what that oven was bought for.
= 那台烤箱买来就是为了烤鱼柳配豌豆。
fish fingers = 鱼柳条
⑥ Finger food and a finger bowl belong to two different kinds of evening.
= 手抓小食和洗手盅，属于两种不同的晚宴。
finger food = 手抓小食
a finger bowl = 洗手盅
⑦ Finger-painting covers the child, the table and one entire wall.
= 手指画会涂满孩子、桌子，还有整整一面墙。
finger-painting = 手指画
⑧ The report is finger-pointing dressed up as analysis.
= 那份报告是相互指责，装成了分析。
finger-pointing = 相互指责

fingerboard
核心：（乐器）指板：小提琴、吉他上按弦的那块木板。可数。
① The fingerboard is worn where four generations have put the same finger.
= 指板上磨出一处凹痕，四代人都把同一根手指按在那儿。
a fingerboard = 指板
② A warped fingerboard makes every note above the fifth fret sour.
= 指板一变形，五品以上的音全走调。
a warped fingerboard = 变形的指板

fingering
核心：（音乐）指法：乐谱上标着哪个音该用哪根手指。不可数。
① The fingering in that edition suits a hand that nobody actually has.
= 那个版本上的指法，适合一双谁也没有的手。
fingering = 指法
② She pencils her own fingering over the printed one and rubs it out yearly.
= 她在印好的指法上用铅笔另标一套，每年擦掉重来。
mark the fingering = 标出指法

fingerless
核心：无指的：几乎只用在 fingerless gloves 上，就是露指手套。
① Fingerless gloves keep the palms warm and the fingers ready.
= 露指手套让手心暖着，手指随时能用。
fingerless gloves = 露指手套
② A fingerless mitten sounds like a compromise and works like one.
= 露指的连指手套听着像折中，用起来也是。
fingerless = 无指的

fingermark
核心：（英式）指印、手指留下的污痕：多说玻璃与门上的那种。可数。
① Fingermarks on the glass show up only when the sun is low.
= 玻璃上的手指印，只有太阳低的时候才看得见。
a fingermark = 指印污痕
② She cleaned the door twice and left two fingermarks doing it.
= 她把门擦了两遍，擦的时候又留下两个指印。
leave a fingermark = 留下指印

fingernail
核心：手指甲。可数。
① A broken fingernail ended the recital and nobody blamed her for it.
= 一片指甲断了，独奏会就此中止，没人怪她。
a fingernail = 手指甲
② He bites his fingernails in meetings and denies it in the corridor.
= 他开会时咬指甲，出了会议室就不认。
bite your fingernails = 咬指甲

fingerprint
核心：指纹；也作动词，给人采指纹。可数。名词 fingerprinting。
① A fingerprint on the inside of the window took the case somewhere new.
= 窗户内侧的一枚指纹，把案子引向了新的方向。
a fingerprint = 指纹
② Everybody entering the site is fingerprinted and photographed twice.
= 进那片场地的人都要采指纹，还要拍两张照。
be fingerprinted = 被采指纹

fingerprinting
核心：采集指纹这件事：不可数；genetic fingerprinting 指基因图谱鉴定。
① Fingerprinting takes four minutes and the paperwork takes forty.
= 采指纹四分钟，文书要四十分钟。
fingerprinting = 采集指纹
② Genetic fingerprinting settled a question that had run for thirty years.
= 基因图谱鉴定，了结了一个拖了三十年的问题。
genetic fingerprinting = 基因图谱鉴定

fingertip
核心：指尖。可数。习语 at your fingertips 是随手可得、了如指掌。
① The switch is exactly where your fingertip lands if you know that room.
= 你要是熟那间屋子，指尖落下的地方正好是开关。
a fingertip = 指尖
② She has the timetable at her fingertips and cannot find her own keys.
= 那张时刻表她了如指掌，自己的钥匙却找不着。
at your fingertips = 随手可得、了如指掌

finial
核心：（建筑）顶饰：塔尖、栏杆柱、家具顶端那个装饰的小尖头。可数。
① The finial came off in the gale and turned up in a neighbour's pond.
= 那个顶饰在大风里掉了，后来在邻居的池塘里找到。
a finial = 顶饰
② Each railing has a finial and three of them are the wrong shape.
= 每根栏杆都有个顶饰，其中三个形状不对。
a carved finial = 雕花顶饰

finicky
核心：（贬）过分挑剔的；也说活儿琐碎难弄。
① He is finicky about tea and drinks whatever is in the pot at work.
= 他对茶挑剔得很，上班时壶里有什么就喝什么。
be finicky about something = 对某事挑剔
② The wiring is finicky and takes small hands and good light.
= 那些接线活儿琐碎，要手小，还要光线好。
a finicky job = 琐碎难做的活

finings
核心：（酿造）澄清剂：加进酒或啤酒里、让杂质沉底的东西。作复数。
① Finings pull the haze out of the beer in about two days.
= 澄清剂两天上下就把啤酒里的浑浊拉下去。
finings = 澄清剂
② Some finings come from fish and the label rarely says so.
= 有些澄清剂取自鱼，标签上很少写出来。
add finings = 加入澄清剂

finish
核心：四支。一是完成、结束：finish the job；二是（比赛的）冲线与名次：a close finish；
三是（表面的）漆面、饰面：a matt finish；四是短语动词 finish off 与 finish up。
① Finish the job before the light goes and the ladder can stay out.
= 天黑之前把活干完，梯子就能搁在外头。
finish the job = 把活干完
finish doing something = 做完某事
② A close finish sent everybody back to look at the photograph.
= 那场比赛冲线极近，人人又回去看照片。
a close finish = 极接近的终点
③ The table has a matt finish that hides everything except water.
= 那张桌子是哑光漆面，什么都遮得住，唯独遮不住水。
a matt finish = 哑光漆面
④ They finished off the paint and finished up with two different whites.
= 他们把漆用完了，最后手上剩两种不同的白。
finish something off = 把某物用完、收尾
finish up = 最后落得
⑤ She finished with that job before it could finish with her.
= 她先跟那份工作了断，没等它先了断她。
finish with something = 跟某事了断

finished
核心：三支。一是完成了的：a finished product；二是（人）完了、不行了；
三是（表面）加工过的。
① The finished product weighs less than the box it arrives in.
= 成品比装它的那个箱子还轻。
a finished product = 成品
② After that vote he was finished and the party took three years to notice.
= 那次表决之后他就完了，党内花了三年才察觉。
be finished = 完蛋了

finisher
核心：两支。一是跑完全程的人：多见于赛事报道；
二是（工艺）做最后加工的人或机器。可数。
① Every finisher gets a medal and the last one gets the loudest cheer.
= 每个跑完全程的人都有奖牌，最后那个得到的欢呼最响。
a finisher = 完赛者
② The finisher smooths the concrete before it goes off and never gets photographed.
= 那台抹光机在混凝土凝固前把它抹平，从没人给它拍过照。
a finisher = 抹光机、精加工工人

finite
核心：有限的、有尽头的：finite resources；语法上也说限定的动词形式。
反义 infinite。
① Finite resources and an infinite committee make for a very long year.
= 资源有限，委员会却没完没了，这一年就格外长。
finite resources = 有限的资源
② A finite number of chairs meets an unpredictable number of guests.
= 数目固定的椅子，碰上人数说不准的客人。
a finite number = 有限的数目

fink
核心：（美式，俚语，贬）告密者、卑鄙小人：也作动词，fink on somebody 是告发某人。
① Nobody in that yard would work beside a fink, whatever the pay was.
= 那个厂区里，不管给多少钱，谁也不肯跟告密的人一起干。
a fink = 告密者
② He finked on two colleagues and kept the job for another decade.
= 他告发了两名同事，那份工作又干了十年。
fink on somebody = 告发某人

Finn
核心：芬兰人：芬兰的国民；形容词与语言都是 Finnish。可数。
① A Finn on that team skis to training when the road is closed.
= 队里那个芬兰人，路一封就滑雪去训练。
a Finn = 芬兰人
the Finns = 芬兰人（整体）
② Two Finns and a Swede shared a cabin and one dictionary.
= 两个芬兰人加一个瑞典人合住一间小屋，共用一本词典。

finnan
核心：（苏格兰菜）冷熏黑线鳕：全称 finnan haddock 或 finnan haddie，
冷熏而不上色。
① Finnan haddock poached in milk is breakfast in that part of the country.
= 牛奶煮冷熏黑线鳕，在那一带就是早饭。
finnan haddock = 冷熏黑线鳕
② A finnan is smoked cold and stays the colour of the fish itself.
= 这种熏鱼是冷熏的，颜色还是鱼本来的颜色。
a finnan = 冷熏黑线鳕

Finnish
核心：芬兰的、芬兰语：形容词与语言名；说人时用 Finn。
① Finnish has fifteen cases and no future tense at all.
= 芬兰语有十五个格，根本没有将来时。
Finnish = 芬兰语
② The Finnish team arrived a day early and trained in the rain.
= 芬兰队早到一天，在雨里练了一场。
the Finnish team = 芬兰队

fintech
核心：金融科技：拿技术做金融服务的那个行当，也指这类公司。不可数为主。
① Fintech promised to kill the branch and killed the queue instead.
= 金融科技扬言要干掉网点，结果干掉的是排队。
fintech = 金融科技
② A fintech start-up runs on two people and a great deal of compliance.
= 一家金融科技初创公司，靠两个人加一大堆合规撑着。
a fintech company = 金融科技公司

fiord
核心：峡湾：fjord 的另一种拼法，指冰川挖出来的深长海湾。可数。
① The fiord is nine hundred metres deep and the ferry crosses it in eleven minutes.
= 那道峡湾深九百米，渡轮十一分钟就横渡过去。
a fiord = 峡湾
the head of a fiord = 峡湾的尽头
② Snow on both sides of the fiord lasts until the middle of June.
= 峡湾两岸的雪，一直留到六月中旬。

fir
核心：冷杉：常绿针叶树，针叶扁平，球果朝上直立。可数。
① A fir planted in 1971 now blocks the light from two windows.
= 1971 年栽下的一棵冷杉，如今挡住了两扇窗的光。
a fir = 冷杉
② Fir cones sit upright on the branch and fall apart rather than drop.
= 冷杉球果直立在枝上，是散开的，不是整个掉下来。
a fir cone = 冷杉球果

fire
核心：五支。一是火、火灾：put out a fire；二是（英式）取暖炉具：an electric fire；
三是开火射击；四是解雇：be fired；
五是一串习语：catch fire、set fire to、on fire、under fire、open fire、play with fire。
① It took nine minutes to put out a fire that had started in a bin.
= 一场从垃圾桶烧起来的火，扑了九分钟。
put out a fire = 扑灭火
make a fire = 生火
a house fire = 住宅火灾
② The curtains caught fire and the smoke alarm stayed silent throughout.
= 窗帘着了火，烟雾报警器全程一声没响。
catch fire = 着火
③ Somebody set fire to the shed and left the door open behind them.
= 有人放火烧了那间棚子，走的时候门还敞着。
set fire to something = 放火烧某物
④ The whole hillside was on fire by four and out by midnight.
= 到四点整面山坡都在烧，午夜前扑灭。
on fire = 着火、在燃烧
⑤ The minister came under fire for a sentence she had not written.
= 那位大臣因一句不是她写的话挨了炮轰。
come under fire = 受到抨击
⑥ They opened fire on an empty ridge and the report says as much.
= 他们朝一道空无一人的山脊开了火，报告里也是这么写的。
open fire = 开火
⑦ Two people were fired and the third resigned before the meeting started.
= 有两个人被解雇，第三个在开会前就辞了。
be fired = 被解雇
⑧ Lending your name to that scheme is playing with fire.
= 把名字借给那个方案，是玩火。
play with fire = 玩火
⑨ The fire department sent two fire engines and a fire truck that would not start.
= 消防局派来两辆消防车，还有一辆打不着火的云梯车。
the fire department = 消防局（美式）
the fire service = 消防部门（英式）
a fire engine = 消防车
a fire truck = 云梯消防车（美式）
⑩ The fire chief runs three stations and the fire company at the harbour runs itself.
= 消防长官管着三个站，港口那支消防队自己管自己。
a fire chief = 消防长官
a fire company = 消防队
a fire station = 消防站
a firehouse = 消防站（美式）
⑪ A fire hose froze to the fire hydrant and took two men to free.
= 一根消防水带冻在消火栓上，两个人才弄开。
a fire hose = 消防水带
a fire hydrant = 消火栓
⑫ The fire escape ends a floor above the ground and the building is a fire trap.
= 那道太平梯到二楼就断了，整栋楼就是个火灾陷阱。
a fire escape = 太平梯
a fire trap = 极易失火的房子
⑬ The door is fireproof, the curtain is fire-retardant, and the fireguard is missing.
= 门是防火的，帘子是阻燃的，炉前围栏却不见了。
fireproof = 防火的
fire-retardant = 阻燃的
a fireguard = 炉前围栏
a fire screen = 炉前挡板
⑭ Fire practice happens twice a year and the fire line gets repainted after each one.
= 消防演习一年两次，每次之后那条消防警戒线都要重画。
fire practice = 消防演习
a fire line = 消防警戒线
⑮ The fire-raiser was caught in April and fire-raising in that county carries seven years.
= 那名纵火者四月落网，在那个郡纵火要判七年。
a fire-raiser = 纵火者
fire-raising = 纵火
⑯ The fire-eater works for tips and buys a firelighter every Saturday.
= 那位吞火艺人靠打赏过活，每周六买一块引火块。
a fire-eater = 吞火艺人
a firelighter = 引火块
a fire starter = 引火物
⑰ Firewood stacked against that wall dries in a summer and burns in a week.
= 靠那面墙码着的柴火，一个夏天晾干，一个星期烧完。
firewood = 柴火
⑱ Firelight from the fireplace reaches the fireside chair and stops there.
= 壁炉的火光照到炉边那把椅子，就到那儿为止。
a fireplace = 壁炉
firelight = 火光
the fireside = 炉边
⑲ A firestorm of criticism followed a decision that had taken nine minutes.
= 一个只花了九分钟的决定，引来铺天盖地的批评。
a firestorm = 铺天盖地的风暴
⑳ The firewall blocks that site at work and the same site opens at home.
= 防火墙在单位挡住那个网站，同一个网站在家却打得开。
a firewall = 防火墙
㉑ Fire ants arrived with the topsoil and now own the entire lawn.
= 火蚁是随表土来的，如今整片草坪都归它们。
fire ants = 火蚁
㉒ The shop is having a fire sale and the fireworks are going at half price.
= 那家店在清仓甩卖，烟花半价出。
a fire sale = 清仓甩卖
a firework = 烟花
firepower = 火力
㉓ One glass of that firewater ended the evening for two of them.
= 那种烈酒一杯下肚，他们里有两个的晚上就到此为止。
firewater = 烈酒（俗称）
㉔ Fire away — the meeting has nine minutes left and no agenda at all.
= 你尽管问 —— 这个会还剩九分钟，连议程都没有。
fire away = 尽管问、开始吧
㉕ He fired off three emails and then fired up the printer that always jams.
= 他连发三封邮件，又启动了那台老卡纸的打印机。
fire off = 迅速发出
fire up = 启动、点着
㉖ A firefly in that jar lasted one night and taught nine children something.
= 罐子里那只萤火虫活了一夜，教会九个孩子一件事。
a firefly = 萤火虫
㉗ A fireside chat on the radio reached more people than any speech in that hall.
= 电台里的炉边谈话，比那间礼堂里任何演讲传得都远。
a fireside chat = 炉边谈话

firearm
核心：（正式）枪支：手枪、步枪一类，多用复数 firearms。可数。
① A firearms certificate takes eleven weeks and two referees to obtain.
= 办一张持枪证要十一个星期，还要两位担保人。
a firearms certificate = 持枪证
② The museum keeps the firearm in a case with no label at all.
= 博物馆把那支枪放在一个完全没有标签的柜子里。
a firearm = 枪支

fireball
核心：三支。一是火球：爆炸时腾起的那团火；二是（天文）火流星；
三是（非正式）精力过人的人。可数。
① The fireball took out the windows and the fire itself did far less damage.
= 那团火球震碎了窗户，火本身造成的损失反倒小得多。
a fireball = 火球
② A fireball crossed the sky at nine and the switchboard lit up.
= 九点有颗火流星划过天空，总机电话立刻被打爆。
a fireball = 火流星

fireballer
核心：（棒球，美式）快球投手：球速极快的投手。可数。
① The fireballer throws hard for four innings and has nothing after that.
= 那位快球投手能猛投四局，之后就没东西了。
a fireballer = 快球投手
a young fireballer = 年轻的快球投手
② Every scout wants a fireballer and every coach wants a second pitch.
= 球探个个想要快球投手，教练个个想要第二种球路。

firebomb
核心：燃烧弹：名词与动词同形。可数。
① A firebomb through the letterbox failed to light and the family moved anyway.
= 一枚从信箱塞进来的燃烧弹没点着，那家人还是搬走了。
a firebomb = 燃烧弹
② The depot was firebombed twice in the same summer.
= 那座仓库同一个夏天被投了两次燃烧弹。
be firebombed = 遭燃烧弹袭击

firebrand
核心：煽动者、鼓动家：说话激烈、专会点火的人。可数，褒贬都能用。
① The firebrand of 1968 chairs the pensions committee now.
= 1968 年那位鼓动家，如今主持养老金委员会。
a firebrand = 煽动者
② A firebrand speech filled the hall and emptied the negotiating table.
= 一场激烈的演说挤满了会场，清空了谈判桌。
a firebrand speech = 煽动性的演说

firebreak
核心：防火隔离带：林中特意清出的空带，用来挡住火势。可数。
① The firebreak held on the north side and gave way on the east.
= 那条防火带在北面守住了，东面破了口。
a firebreak = 防火隔离带
② Cutting a firebreak takes two days and can save a whole forest.
= 开一条防火带要两天，能救下整片林子。
cut a firebreak = 开辟防火带

firebrick
核心：耐火砖：炉膛与烟囱里用的耐高温砖。可数。
① The firebricks at the back of the stove outlast everything around them.
= 炉子后壁那些耐火砖，比周围一切都耐用。
a firebrick = 耐火砖
line something with firebrick = 用耐火砖砌衬
② Two cracked firebricks let the heat straight into the wall behind.
= 两块开裂的耐火砖，让热量直接透进后面的墙。

firebug
核心：（非正式）纵火犯：爱放火的人。可数。
① A firebug in the district set nine bins alight in one month.
= 那个区有个纵火的，一个月点了九个垃圾桶。
a firebug = 纵火犯
catch a firebug = 抓住纵火犯
② The firebug was caught by a camera that had not worked for a year.
= 那个纵火犯，被一台坏了一年的摄像头拍下来了。

firecracker
核心：两支。一是爆竹、鞭炮；二是（非正式）活力四射的人。可数。
① A firecracker went off in the yard and the dog is still under the bed.
= 院子里响了个爆竹，狗到现在还在床底下。
a firecracker = 爆竹
② She is a firecracker on stage and very quiet at the table afterwards.
= 她在台上活力四射，散场后在饭桌上安静得很。
a firecracker = 活力四射的人

firefight
核心：（军事）交火：双方持枪对射的战斗。可数。
① The firefight lasted forty minutes and neither side moved a single metre.
= 那场交火打了四十分钟，双方谁也没挪出一米。
a firefight = 交火
② Two firefights in one week emptied the village of everybody who could leave.
= 一个星期里两次交火，能走的人都走光了。
be caught in a firefight = 卷入交火

firefighter
核心：消防员：如今通用的说法，取代了旧称 fireman。可数。
① Firefighters got there in six minutes and the road took four of them.
= 消防员六分钟就到，其中四分钟花在路上。
a firefighter = 消防员
a retained firefighter = 兼职消防员
② Two firefighters carried the ladder that four people had brought.
= 四个人抬来的那架梯子，两名消防员就扛走了。

firefighting
核心：两支。一是灭火这件事；二是引申的四处救火、疲于应付。不可数。
① Firefighting equipment is checked in April and needed in August.
= 灭火器材四月检查，八月用得上。
firefighting equipment = 灭火器材
② The whole job is firefighting and the plan gets written in December.
= 那份工作整天都在救火，计划要到十二月才写得出来。
firefighting = 疲于应付

fireman
核心：两支。一是（旧称）消防员：如今通用 firefighter，这个词多见于旧文；
二是蒸汽机车上的司炉。可数，复数 firemen。
① The fireman on that engine shovelled two tons of coal in a single shift.
= 那台机车上的司炉，一个班要铲两吨煤。
a fireman = 司炉（蒸汽机车上的）
② A fireman in that photograph is holding a hose that needs four men.
= 照片里那位消防员，端着一根要四个人才拿得住的水带。
a fireman = 消防员（旧称）

firing
核心：三支。一是射击：firing practice；二是解雇：the firing of three managers；
三是（陶瓷的）烧制。不可数为主。习语 in the firing line 是首当其冲。
① Firing practice happens at dawn and the sheep have got used to it.
= 打靶训练在天亮时进行，羊群已经习惯了。
firing practice = 打靶训练
in the firing line = 首当其冲
② The firing of three managers in one week emptied the car park by six.
= 一个星期解雇三名经理，六点停车场就空了。
the firing of somebody = 对某人的解雇
③ The second firing sets the glaze and the first only hardens the clay.
= 第二次烧制定住釉面，头一次只是把泥坯烧硬。
a firing = 一次烧制
④ The firing squad in that film is eleven men and one blank round.
= 那部电影里的行刑队，十一个人，一发空包弹。
a firing squad = 行刑队

firkin
核心：（英式，酿造）小桶：约九加仑的啤酒桶。可数。
① A firkin lasts that pub a night and a half.
= 一小桶啤酒，那家酒馆一晚半就卖完。
a firkin = 九加仑小桶
a firkin of ale = 一小桶麦芽啤酒
② Two firkins came off the lorry and one of them was already leaking.
= 两只小桶从货车上卸下来，其中一只已经在漏了。

firm
核心：四支。一是结实的、硬实的：a firm mattress；二是坚定的：be firm with somebody；
三是（日期、安排）定死的：a firm date；四是名词，公司：a law firm。副词 firmly。
① A firm mattress and a soft pillow is the compromise that household reached.
= 硬床垫配软枕头，是那家人达成的折中。
a firm mattress = 硬床垫
② You have to be firm with a puppy and gentle about how you do it.
= 对小狗要坚定，态度还得温和。
be firm with somebody = 对某人态度坚定
③ There is no firm date yet and the invitations are already printed.
= 日子还没定死，请柬倒已经印好了。
a firm date = 确定的日期
④ The law firm has nine partners and one working lift.
= 那家律所有九位合伙人，一部还能用的电梯。
a law firm = 律师事务所
副词 firmly：牢牢地、坚决地，多修饰 shut、held、believed。
⑤ The door was firmly shut and the key was in the other coat.
= 门关得严严实实，钥匙在另一件外套里。
firmly shut = 关得严实
⑥ She firmly believes the second version and has read only the first.
= 她坚信第二个版本，读过的却只有第一个。
firmly believe = 坚信
⑦ Prices firm up in spring and soften again by the middle of August.
= 价格春天走稳，到八月中又松动。
firm up = 走稳、确定下来

firmament
核心：（书面、旧）苍穹、天空；引申指某个领域的「星空」。不可数。
① Stars filled the firmament and the streetlight filled everything else.
= 星星填满苍穹，路灯填满了别的一切。
the firmament = 苍穹
② A new name in the literary firmament lasts about two seasons.
= 文坛星空里的一个新名字，大约亮两季。
the literary firmament = 文坛星空

firmness
核心：两支。一是（质地的）硬实；二是（态度的）坚定。不可数。
① The firmness of that mattress is why he sleeps at his mother's.
= 那张床垫太硬，所以他都去母亲家睡。
the firmness of something = 某物的软硬程度
② Firmness at the first meeting saves three meetings later on.
= 头一次会上态度坚定，能省下后面三次会。
firmness = 坚定

firmware
核心：（计算机）固件：写死在设备里的那层程序，介于硬件与软件之间。不可数。
① A firmware update took the camera offline for a day and fixed nothing.
= 一次固件更新让那台相机停用一天，什么也没修好。
a firmware update = 固件更新
② The firmware in that box is older than the phone that talks to it.
= 那个盒子里的固件，比跟它通信的手机还老。
firmware = 固件

first
核心：五支。一是第一的、最先的：the first time；二是副词，首先；
三是名词，第一次、第一名；四是（英式）一等荣誉学位：get a first；
五是一串习语：at first、first of all、first come, first served。副词 firstly 用于列举。
① The first time she drove that road she went the wrong way twice.
= 她头一回开那条路，走错了两次。
the first time = 第一次
② At first the noise was funny and by Thursday it was not.
= 起初那声音还好笑，到周四就不好笑了。
at first = 起初
③ First of all, nobody asked; second, nobody minded.
= 首先，没人问过；其次，没人在意。
first of all = 首先
④ Seats are first come, first served and the queue starts at seven.
= 座位先到先得，七点就开始排队。
first come, first served = 先到先得
⑤ She got a first and now works in a shop she actually likes.
= 她拿了一等荣誉学位，如今在一家自己真心喜欢的店里上班。
get a first = 拿到一等荣誉学位
副词 firstly：列举时的「第一」，跟 secondly 成套，用在正式行文里。
⑥ Firstly the roof, secondly the boiler, and thirdly the money.
= 第一是屋顶，第二是锅炉，第三是钱。
firstly = 第一（列举用语）
⑦ First aid was given by a first-aider who had qualified the week before.
= 急救是由一位上星期才拿到证的急救员做的。
first aid = 急救
a first-aider = 急救员
a first responder = 首批到场的急救人员
⑧ The first floor in Britain is the second floor in America and the lift knows neither.
= 英国说的「first floor」是美国的二楼，电梯两边都不认。
the first floor = （英式）二楼、（美式）一楼
a first balcony = 剧场二层前排包厢
⑨ Her first name is on the badge and nobody uses it.
= 她的名字印在胸牌上，没人用。
a first name = 名字
a first language = 母语
⑩ The first night went well and the first-timer in row three cried twice.
= 首演之夜很顺利，第三排那位头一回来的观众哭了两次。
a first night = 首演之夜
a first-timer = 头一回做某事的人
a first-time buyer = 首次购房者
⑪ A first offender gets a warning and a first-degree charge gets a lawyer.
= 初犯者领一句警告，一级重罪指控要请律师。
a first offender = 初犯者
a first degree = 一级罪；学士学位
first blood = 首次得手
⑫ At first light the first mate was already on deck and the first officer was not.
= 天刚亮，大副已经在甲板上，二副还没有。
first light = 破晓
a first mate = 大副
a first officer = 副驾驶、二副
a first lieutenant = 中尉
a first sergeant = 一级军士长
⑬ The first minister answered on first principles and satisfied nobody.
= 那位首席大臣从最根本的原则答起，谁也没说服。
a first minister = 首席大臣
first principles = 最根本的原则
⑭ A first-rate cast and a first-ever staging filled the house for a fortnight.
= 一流的阵容加史上头一回搬演，把剧场坐满了两个星期。
first-rate = 一流的
first-ever = 有史以来头一回的
first-hand = 第一手的
⑮ First-past-the-post gives one seat to whoever comes first and nothing to the rest.
= 简单多数制把席位给跑在最前的那个，别人一无所得。
first-past-the-post = 简单多数选举制
first refusal = 优先购买权
first reading = （议案的）一读
⑯ The first-generation machines still work and the first-day covers are worth more.
= 第一代机器还能用，首日封反倒更值钱。
first generation = 第一代
a first-day cover = 首日封
first cost = 初始成本
⑰ He plays first base for a club whose first string trains on Thursdays.
= 他为一家俱乐部打一垒，那家俱乐部的主力阵容周四训练。
first base = 一垒
first string = 主力阵容
⑱ The child moved from first school to the next one halfway through the year.
= 那孩子读到学年一半，就从初级小学转去了下一所。
a first school = 初级小学
⑲ A first strike would end the treaty and the first-line units know it.
= 先发制人的一击会终结那份条约，一线部队心里有数。
a first strike = 先发制人的打击
first-line = 一线的
⑳ First-class cricket starts in April and the first fruit of the season is a draw.
= 一级板球四月开赛，本季头一个成果是一场平局。
first-class cricket = 一级板球赛
a first fruit = 头一批成果
㉑ They first-foot the neighbours at midnight and the first-footer carries coal.
= 他们午夜去邻家「初进门」，头一个进门的人手里捧着煤。
first-foot = （苏格兰）新年头一个进门拜年
a first-footer = 新年头一个进门的人
㉒ The first finger on that hand is shorter than the second and always has been.
= 那只手的食指比中指短，一向如此。
the first finger = 食指
㉓ A first-person shooter sold nine million copies and taught nobody to shoot.
= 一款第一人称射击游戏卖了九百万份，没教会谁开枪。
a first-person shooter = 第一人称射击游戏
㉔ The first down came in the third minute and the crowd sat back down.
= 第一次进攻推进出现在第三分钟，观众又坐了回去。
a first down = （美式足球）首攻成功

firstborn
核心：长子、长女：家里第一个出生的孩子。可数，也作定语。
① The firstborn gets the photographs and the second gets the hand-me-downs.
= 老大占了照片，老二捡旧衣裳。
the firstborn = 长子、长女
② Their firstborn was named after a street and the second after a saint.
= 他们的老大用一条街的名字命名，老二用了一位圣人的。
a firstborn son = 长子

firth
核心：（苏格兰）河口湾：河流入海处那片宽阔水域，多见于地名。可数。
① The firth is grey in every photograph and blue about four days a year.
= 那道河口湾在每张照片里都是灰的，一年大约有四天是蓝的。
a firth = 河口湾
cross the firth = 横渡河口湾
② A bridge across the firth took nine years and two different designs.
= 跨那道河口湾的桥，修了九年，换过两版设计。

fiscal
核心：（正式）财政的、税收的：fiscal policy、the fiscal year。副词 fiscally。
① Fiscal policy moves slowly and everybody feels it at the same moment.
= 财政政策动得慢，人人却在同一刻感觉到。
fiscal policy = 财政政策
② The fiscal year ends in April and the paperwork ends in June.
= 财政年度四月结束，文书到六月才完。
the fiscal year = 财政年度
副词 fiscally：在财政上，多说 fiscally neutral、fiscally responsible。
③ The plan is fiscally neutral and politically expensive.
= 那个方案财政上不增负担，政治上代价高昂。
fiscally neutral = 财政上中性的
④ A fiscally responsible budget still needs somebody to vote for it.
= 一份财政上稳健的预算，也还得有人投票通过。
fiscally responsible = 财政上稳健的
⑤ The fiscal cliff arrived on schedule and nobody had packed for it.
= 财政悬崖如期而至，谁都没为它做准备。
a fiscal cliff = 财政悬崖

fish
核心：四支。一是鱼：单复数同形，说不同种类时用 fishes；二是鱼肉：fish and chips；
三是动词，钓鱼、捕鱼；四是习语：a fish out of water 是浑身不自在的人。
① Three fish came out of that pond in a summer and two went back in.
= 那口塘一夏天钓上来三条鱼，放回去两条。
a fish = 一条鱼
② Fish and chips on a Friday is a habit older than that shop.
= 周五吃炸鱼薯条，这习惯比那家店还老。
fish and chips = 炸鱼薯条
③ He fishes the same stretch of river every Sunday and catches almost nothing.
= 他每个周日钓同一段河，几乎什么也钓不着。
go fishing = 去钓鱼
④ She was a fish out of water at that dinner and left before the pudding.
= 那顿饭上她浑身不自在，甜点还没上就走了。
a fish out of water = 格格不入的人
⑤ The fish and chip shop opens at five and the queue starts at half past four.
= 那家炸鱼薯条店五点开门，四点半就开始排队。
a fish and chip shop = 炸鱼薯条店
⑥ A fish knife and a fish slice look alike to everybody except the washer-up.
= 鱼刀跟煎鱼铲长得像，只有洗碗的人分得清。
a fish knife = 鱼刀
a fish slice = 煎鱼铲
⑦ He fished for a compliment and nobody at the table took the bait.
= 他拐弯抹角讨夸奖，桌上没人上钩。
fish for something = 拐弯抹角地讨要某物
⑧ A fish hook through a finger ends a trip faster than rain does.
= 鱼钩扎进手指，比下雨更快地结束一趟出行。
a fish hook = 鱼钩
a fish stick = 鱼柳条（美式）

fishbowl
核心：两支。一是鱼缸；二是引申，毫无隐私可言的处境。可数。
① The fishbowl on the counter holds one fish and a great deal of gravel.
= 柜台上那只鱼缸养着一条鱼，铺了一大堆石子。
a fishbowl = 鱼缸
② Living in a fishbowl is the part of the job nobody warns you about.
= 活得像在鱼缸里，是这份工作中没人事先提醒你的那一部分。
live in a fishbowl = 活在众目睽睽之下

fishcake
核心：鱼饼：鱼肉拌土豆泥做成饼，裹粉煎或炸。可数。
① Fishcakes are what happens to yesterday's fish and today's potatoes.
= 鱼饼就是昨天的鱼加今天的土豆的去处。
a fishcake = 鱼饼
a salmon fishcake = 三文鱼饼
② Two fishcakes and a wedge of lemon is that whole lunch menu.
= 两个鱼饼加一角柠檬，就是那份午餐菜单的全部。

fisher
核心：两支。一是（正式或旧）打鱼的人：如今多说 fisherman 或 angler；
二是（北美）渔貂，一种鼬科动物。可数。
① The fisher in that story catches one fish and then lets it talk.
= 那个故事里的渔人钓上一条鱼，还让它开口说话。
a fisher = 打鱼的人
② A fisher is a weasel the size of a cat and twice as unwelcome.
= 渔貂是一种猫那么大的鼬，讨人嫌的程度是猫的两倍。
a fisher = 渔貂

fisherman
核心：渔民、钓鱼的人：复数 fishermen；说女性可用 fisherwoman。可数。
① The fisherman on that boat mends nets faster than he catches anything.
= 那条船上的渔民，补网比打鱼快。
a fisherman = 渔民
a deep-sea fisherman = 远洋渔民
② Two fishermen share a licence and never fish the same tide.
= 两个渔民合用一张执照，从不在同一个潮汛出海。

fisherwoman
核心：女渔民、钓鱼的女人：复数 fisherwomen。可数。
① The fisherwoman at the end of the pier out-fishes everybody with better gear.
= 栈桥尽头那位女钓者，比装备更好的人钓得都多。
a fisherwoman = 女渔民
a local fisherwoman = 本地的女渔民
② Two fisherwomen run that boat and hire help only in August.
= 两位女渔民经营着那条船，只在八月雇帮手。

fishery
核心：两支。一是渔场、渔业区；二是渔业这个行当。可数。
① The fishery closed for two seasons and the stock came back in three.
= 那片渔场关了两季，鱼群三季之后才回来。
a fishery = 渔场
② Fishery policy is decided a long way from any actual boat.
= 渔业政策，是在离任何一条真船都很远的地方定下的。
fishery policy = 渔业政策

fisheye
核心：（摄影）鱼眼的：a fisheye lens 指视角极广、画面外凸的镜头。
① A fisheye lens makes a small room look like a stadium.
= 鱼眼镜头能把一间小屋拍得像体育场。
a fisheye lens = 鱼眼镜头
② The fisheye view bends every straight line in that building.
= 鱼眼视角把那栋楼里的每条直线都拗弯了。
a fisheye view = 鱼眼视角

fishing
核心：钓鱼、捕鱼：不可数 —— go fishing、a fishing rod、fishing rights。
① Fishing on that stretch needs a permit and a great deal of patience.
= 在那一段钓鱼，要有许可证，还要极大的耐心。
a fishing rod = 钓竿
② Fishing rights on the river were sold in 1911 and never bought back.
= 那条河的捕鱼权 1911 年卖掉了，再没买回来。
fishing rights = 捕鱼权
③ The fishing tackle fills a shed and the fishing line is always the wrong weight.
= 渔具塞满一间棚子，鱼线的号数总是不对。
fishing tackle = 渔具
a fishing line = 鱼线
④ The inquiry turned into a fishing expedition and found one parking fine.
= 那场调查变成了漫无目标的搜罗，只查出一张停车罚单。
a fishing expedition = 漫无目标的搜罗

fishmeal
核心：鱼粉：小鱼磨成的粉，做饲料与肥料。不可数。
① Fishmeal feeds the salmon that people pay a great deal to eat.
= 鱼粉喂大了那些人们花大价钱吃的三文鱼。
fishmeal = 鱼粉
② The smell of fishmeal reaches the village whenever the wind turns.
= 风向一转，鱼粉的味道就飘进村里。
a fishmeal factory = 鱼粉厂

fishmonger
核心：（英式）鱼贩，也指鱼店：卖鱼的人或那家铺子。可数。
① The fishmonger closes at two and knows what everybody wants by name.
= 那家鱼店两点关门，谁要什么，老板都叫得出名字。
a fishmonger = 鱼贩
the fishmonger's = 鱼店
② Two fishmongers in that street became one and then became a café.
= 那条街上两家鱼店并成一家，后来变成了咖啡馆。

fishnet
核心：两支。一是渔网；二是网眼织物：fishnet tights 就是网袜。
① Fishnet tights and a winter coat is a combination that town understands.
= 网袜配冬大衣，那个镇上的人懂这套搭配。
fishnet tights = 网袜
② An old fishnet hangs on the wall and holds up the whole display.
= 一张旧渔网挂在墙上，整面陈列都靠它撑着。
a fishnet = 渔网

fishtail
核心：两支。一是（车）尾部甩摆：后轮打滑时车尾左右扫；
二是鱼尾状的：a fishtail skirt、a fishtail parka。名词与动词同形。
① The van fishtailed on the ice and stopped facing the way it had come.
= 那辆货车在冰面上甩了尾，停下时正对着来路。
fishtail = 车尾甩摆
② A fishtail skirt is easy to walk in and impossible to sit down in.
= 鱼尾裙走路方便，坐下就没法坐了。
a fishtail skirt = 鱼尾裙

fishwife
核心：两支。一是（旧）卖鱼的女人；二是（贬）泼辣骂街的女人：
说话像 fishwife 是嗓门大、话难听。复数 fishwives。
① The fishwife on that corner outsold the shop behind her for thirty years.
= 街角那位卖鱼的女人，三十年里卖得比她身后那家店还多。
a fishwife = 卖鱼的女人
② He said she screeched like a fishwife and lost the argument on the spot.
= 他说她嚷得像个泼妇，当场就把那场争论输掉了。
screech like a fishwife = 像泼妇一样尖声嚷嚷

fishy
核心：两支。一是有鱼味的：a fishy smell；二是（非正式）可疑的：
something fishy 指事情不对劲。
① A fishy smell in the corridor turned out to be the lift motor.
= 走廊里那股鱼腥味，原来是电梯电机发出来的。
a fishy smell = 鱼腥味
② There is something fishy about an invoice with no address on it.
= 一张不写地址的发票，怎么看都不对劲。
something fishy = 可疑的事

fissile
核心：两支。一是（物理）易裂变的：能发生核裂变，如铀 235；
二是（地质）易劈开成薄片的。
① Fissile material is counted, weighed and argued over at every inspection.
= 易裂变材料每次检查都要清点、称重，还要吵上一架。
fissile material = 易裂变材料
② The slate is fissile and splits into sheets a millimetre thick.
= 那种板岩容易劈开，能剥成一毫米厚的薄片。
fissile = 易劈开的

fission
核心：两支。一是（物理）核裂变：nuclear fission，跟聚变 fusion 相对；
二是（生物）分裂生殖。不可数。
① Nuclear fission splits a heavy atom and fusion joins two light ones.
= 核裂变把重原子劈开，聚变把两个轻原子并起来。
nuclear fission = 核裂变
② Bacteria multiply by fission and do it faster when it is warm.
= 细菌靠分裂繁殖，天一暖分得更快。
fission = 分裂生殖

fissure
核心：（正式）裂隙、裂缝：岩石或皮肤上的窄长裂口；引申指组织内部的裂痕。可数。
① A fissure in the rock takes the rope and holds a climber all afternoon.
= 岩石上一道裂隙夹住绳子，整个下午吊着一名攀登者。
a fissure = 裂隙
② The fissure inside the party widened over one paragraph of a manifesto.
= 党内的裂痕，因纲领里的一段话而扩大。
a fissure in something = 某物内部的裂痕

fissured
核心：布满裂纹的：说岩石、树皮，也说皮肤皲裂。
① The bark is deeply fissured and the tree is younger than it looks.
= 那树皮裂纹很深，树本身比看上去年轻。
deeply fissured = 深深皲裂的
② A fissured landscape swallows the rain and gives none of it back.
= 布满裂隙的地貌把雨水吞下去，一点也不还回来。
fissured = 布满裂纹的

fist
核心：拳头。可数。习语：make a good fist of something 是把某事办得像样，
hand over fist 是（赚钱）大把大把地。
① He made a fist and thought better of it before anybody noticed.
= 他握起拳头，没等人看见就改了主意。
make a fist = 握拳
② She made a good fist of a job she had never been trained for.
= 一份从没受过训练的活儿，她干得相当像样。
make a good fist of something = 把某事办得像样
③ That stall makes money hand over fist for six weeks a year.
= 那个摊子一年有六个星期赚得盆满钵满。
hand over fist = 大把大把地
④ A fist bump replaced the handshake and the fist pump replaced the cheer.
= 碰拳取代了握手，挥拳庆祝取代了欢呼。
a fist bump = 碰拳
a fist pump = 挥拳庆祝
fist-pumping = 挥拳庆祝的

fistful
核心：一把（的量）：攥在手里那么多，多说 a fistful of something。可数。
① He arrived with a fistful of receipts and no order to any of them.
= 他攥着一把收据来，一张都没排过序。
a fistful of something = 一把某物
② A fistful of coins bought a newspaper and a bus ride in 1979.
= 1979 年，一把硬币能买一份报纸加一趟公交。
a fistful of coins = 一把硬币

fisticuffs
核心：（旧或戏谑）拳斗、动手打架：作复数，多用在轻描淡写的说法里。
① The argument stopped short of fisticuffs and went on by letter instead.
= 那场争执没到动手的地步，改用写信继续。
come to fisticuffs = 动起手来
② Fisticuffs in the car park cost both of them their season tickets.
= 停车场里一场拳脚，让两人都丢了季票。
fisticuffs = 拳斗

fistula
核心：（医学）瘘管：两个器官或体腔之间长出来的、本不该有的通道。
可数，复数 fistulas 或 fistulae。
① One fistula healed on its own and the second one needed surgery.
= 一处瘘管自己长好了，第二处得动手术。
a fistula = 瘘管
② Fistula repair is routine surgery in one country and rare in another.
= 瘘管修补在一个国家是常规手术，在另一个国家很少见。
fistula repair = 瘘管修补

fit
核心：五支。一是（尺寸）合身：the coat fits；二是健康结实的：keep fit；
三是适合的：fit for purpose；四是名词，（病或情绪的）发作：a coughing fit；
五是习语：see fit 是认为合适，fit in 是融入。
过去式与过去分词英式用 fitted、美式用 fit。
① The coat fits everywhere except across the shoulders.
= 那件大衣哪儿都合身，就是肩那儿不行。
fit somebody = 合某人的身
② She keeps fit by cycling to a gym she never actually goes into.
= 她靠骑车去一家从来不进门的健身房来保持体能。
keep fit = 保持健康
③ The building is not fit for purpose and the sign says it opened in 2019.
= 那栋楼不堪使用，牌子上写着 2019 年启用。
fit for purpose = 合用的
④ A coughing fit ended the speech four minutes early.
= 一阵咳嗽，让那场讲话提前四分钟结束。
a coughing fit = 一阵咳嗽
⑤ Do as you see fit and tell me about it afterwards.
= 你看着办，事后跟我说一声。
see fit = 认为合适
⑥ He fits in everywhere and belongs nowhere in particular.
= 他到哪儿都合得来，哪儿都不算是他的地方。
fit in = 融入
⑦ They fitted out the van in a week and it fits into the garage by nine millimetres.
= 他们一个星期就把货车改装好，进车库只多出九毫米余地。
fit out = 装备、改装
fit into something = 塞进某处
⑧ He was fitted up for something he could not possibly have done.
= 他被人栽赃了一件他绝无可能做的事。
fit somebody up = 栽赃某人
fit up = 栽赃、诬陷

fitful
核心：断断续续的、时有时无的：多说 sleep、progress、rain。副词 fitfully。
① Fitful sleep in a hot room is worse than no sleep in a cold one.
= 在热屋子里睡得断断续续，比在冷屋子里干脆不睡还难受。
fitful sleep = 断断续续的睡眠
② Progress has been fitful and the deadline has not moved at all.
= 进展一直断断续续，截止日期却纹丝没动。
fitful progress = 时断时续的进展
副词 fitfully：断断续续地，多修饰 slept、burned、rained。
③ The lamp burned fitfully and went out whenever the door opened.
= 那盏灯忽明忽暗，一开门就灭。
burn fitfully = 忽明忽暗地燃烧
④ She slept fitfully and got up before the alarm went off anyway.
= 她睡得断断续续，闹钟没响就起来了。
sleep fitfully = 断断续续地睡

fitment
核心：（英式）固定装置、配件：装在墙上、车里或厨房里的那类东西。可数。
① The kitchen fitments came in nine boxes and one of them held the handles.
= 那套厨房配件装了九个箱子，把手全在其中一箱里。
a fitment = 固定装置
② Every fitment on that list costs extra and none of them is optional.
= 那张单子上每样配件都要另加钱，一样都不能不要。
kitchen fitments = 厨房固定配件

fitness
核心：两支。一是健康、体能：physical fitness；
二是（正式）适合、胜任：fitness for something。不可数。
① Physical fitness fades in three weeks and takes three months to build.
= 体能三个星期就掉，练回来要三个月。
physical fitness = 身体素质
② Her fitness for the post was never in doubt and the interview took an hour.
= 她能否胜任那个职位从没人怀疑，面试还是谈了一个钟头。
fitness for something = 对某事的胜任
③ The fitness centre sells a fitness tracker that counts stairs as running.
= 那家健身中心卖一种运动手环，把爬楼算成跑步。
a fitness centre = 健身中心
a fitness tracker = 运动手环

fitted
核心：三支。一是合身的、量身做的：a fitted jacket；
二是（家具）嵌入式的：a fitted kitchen；三是装有某物的：be fitted with something。
① A fitted jacket hides nothing and forgives even less.
= 修身外套什么都藏不住，更谈不上遮丑。
a fitted jacket = 修身外套
② The flat has a fitted kitchen and a bathroom you can just turn round in.
= 那套房有整体厨房，浴室勉强转得开身。
a fitted kitchen = 整体厨房
③ Every room is fitted with a smoke alarm and two of them actually work.
= 每个房间都装了烟雾报警器，真能用的有两个。
be fitted with something = 装有某物
④ The fitted carpet came with the flat and predates the lease by two decades.
= 那块满铺地毯是随房子来的，比租约早二十年。
a fitted carpet = 满铺地毯

fitter
核心：（英式）安装工、装配工；也指量体裁衣的师傅。可数。
① The fitter came twice and the boiler worked on the third visit.
= 安装工来了两趟，第三趟锅炉才好使。
a fitter = 安装工
② A fitter measured her for the dress and took forty minutes over one sleeve.
= 裁缝师傅给她量那条裙子，光一只袖子就量了四十分钟。
a fitter = 量体裁衣的师傅

fitting
核心：三支。一是（服装的）试穿：go for a fitting；二是配件、装置：light fittings；
三是形容词，恰如其分的：a fitting tribute。
① She went for a fitting and the dress came back a different colour.
= 她去试穿，裙子回来时换了个颜色。
go for a fitting = 去试穿
② The light fittings are original and the wiring behind them is not.
= 那些灯具是原装的，后面的线路可不是。
light fittings = 灯具配件
③ A fitting tribute would have been a bench and they built an arch.
= 恰如其分的纪念本该是一条长椅，他们造了一座拱门。
a fitting tribute = 恰如其分的纪念
④ The fitting room has one hook and a mirror that leans forward.
= 那间试衣间只有一个挂钩，还有一面往前倾的镜子。
a fitting room = 试衣间

five
核心：五：基数词；也指五个一组的东西，或（英式口语）五镑钞票。
① Five people can sit at that table and six regularly do.
= 那张桌子坐得下五个人，实际常坐六个。
five = 五
② She handed over a five and got four coins back.
= 她递过去一张五镑，找回四枚硬币。
a five = 五镑钞票
③ Five-a-side is played on a pitch about the size of the car park.
= 五人制足球，就在停车场那么大的场地上踢。
five-a-side = 五人制足球
④ Five-spice goes into the marinade and the rest of the jar sits for a year.
= 五香粉进腌料，剩下的一罐搁上一年。
five-spice = 五香粉
⑤ The five-star rating on that door is nine years old.
= 那扇门上的五星评级，已经是九年前的了。
five-star = 五星级的
⑥ Five pence buys nothing and the five-and-dime that sold it has closed.
= 五便士什么也买不着，卖过那些东西的廉价百货店也关了。
five pence = 五便士
a five-and-dime = 廉价百货店（美式）
⑦ A five o'clock shadow by two in the afternoon runs in that family.
= 下午两点就冒出的胡茬，是那家人的遗传。
a five o'clock shadow = 傍晚冒出的胡茬
a five-o-clock-shadow = 同一个说法的连字符写法

fivefold
核心：五倍的、五倍地：形容词与副词同形。
① A fivefold increase in applications broke the online form twice.
= 申请量涨到五倍，把在线表格搞崩了两回。
a fivefold increase = 五倍的增长
② Costs rose fivefold and the fee went up by four per cent.
= 成本涨了五倍，收费只提了百分之四。
rise fivefold = 涨到五倍

fiver
核心：（英式，非正式）五镑钞票；在美式里指五美元。可数。
① A fiver used to cover lunch and a bus fare in that town.
= 在那个镇上，五镑从前够一顿午饭加一趟公交。
a fiver = 五镑钞票
② He owes her a fiver from 2014 and mentions it every Christmas.
= 他从 2014 年起就欠她五镑，每年圣诞都要提一次。
lend somebody a fiver = 借给某人五镑

fives
核心：（英式）掌球戏：戴手套用手把球打向墙壁的球类游戏，多见于公学。不可数。
① Fives is played against a wall with gloves and no racquet at all.
= 掌球戏是戴着手套对墙打的，根本不用球拍。
fives = 掌球戏
② The fives court is used for storage eleven months of the year.
= 那个掌球场一年有十一个月拿来堆东西。
a fives court = 掌球场

fix
核心：五支。一是修理：fix a leak；二是固定、安上：fix something to something；
三是安排、定下：fix a date；四是（贬）做手脚、内定；
五是名词：in a fix 是陷入困境，a quick fix 是权宜之计。
① He fixed the leak with something that was never meant for pipes.
= 他用一样根本不是给管子用的东西把漏补上了。
fix a leak = 修好漏水
② Fix the shelf to the wall before you put anything heavy on it.
= 放重东西之前，先把架子固定到墙上。
fix something to something = 把某物固定在某处
③ They fixed a date and then moved it twice before the invitations went out.
= 他们定了日子，请柬发出去之前又改了两回。
fix a date = 定下日期
④ The match was fixed and the only surprise was who admitted it.
= 那场比赛是做了手脚的，唯一意外的是谁承认了。
be fixed = 被做了手脚
⑤ We are in a fix and a quick fix will only make it worse.
= 我们陷在困境里，权宜之计只会让事情更糟。
in a fix = 陷入困境
a quick fix = 权宜之计
⑥ They fixed on a date and fixed up the spare room in one afternoon.
= 他们定下日子，一个下午就把空房收拾停当。
fix on something = 定下某事
fix something up = 把某物收拾停当
fix up = 收拾停当、安排好

fixable
核心：修得好的、有办法解决的：多用在否定或对比里。
① The gearbox is fixable and the bodywork is not worth the paint.
= 变速箱修得好，车身连漆钱都不值。
fixable = 修得好的
② A fixable problem left alone for a year stops being fixable.
= 一个本来修得好的问题，撂上一年就修不好了。
a fixable problem = 有办法解决的问题

fixated
核心：（贬）执迷于某事的：be fixated on something，眼里只剩那一件事。
① He is fixated on a rule that was withdrawn back in 2011.
= 他死盯着一条 2011 年就废止了的规矩。
be fixated on something = 执迷于某事
② A committee fixated on process produces minutes and nothing else.
= 一个执迷于流程的委员会，除了会议记录什么也产不出。
fixated = 执迷的

fixation
核心：（贬或心理学）执念、固恋：a fixation with something 或 on something。可数。
① His fixation with the timetable outlasted the service it described.
= 他对那份时刻表的执念，比时刻表上那条线还长命。
a fixation with something = 对某事的执念
② A childhood fixation on trains became a career and then a hobby again.
= 童年对火车的迷恋成了职业，后来又变回爱好。
a fixation on something = 对某事的固恋

fixative
核心：两支。一是（美术）定画液：喷在炭笔或粉彩画上防蹭；
二是香水与标本用的固定剂。可数不可数都行。
① A coat of fixative keeps the charcoal on the paper and dulls it slightly.
= 喷一层定画液，能把炭粉固在纸上，颜色会稍稍发闷。
a fixative = 定画液
② The fixative in that perfume is what makes it last until evening.
= 那款香水里的固香剂，让它一直留到傍晚。
a fixative = 固定剂

fixed
核心：三支。一是固定的、不变的：a fixed price；
二是（表情、目光）僵住不动的：a fixed smile；三是安装牢固的。副词 fixedly。
① A fixed price sounds simple until the extras appear on the second page.
= 固定价格听着简单，直到第二页冒出一堆附加项。
a fixed price = 固定价格
② She kept a fixed smile through nine speeches and one power cut.
= 九段讲话加一次停电，她一直挂着那副僵住的笑。
a fixed smile = 僵住的笑容
副词 fixedly：直勾勾地、目不转睛地，几乎只修饰 stared、gazed、looked。
③ He stared fixedly at the wall behind her and answered every question.
= 他直勾勾地盯着她身后那面墙，把每个问题都答了。
stare fixedly at something = 直勾勾地盯着某物
④ The cat looked fixedly at the cupboard until somebody opened it.
= 那只猫死盯着柜子，直到有人把它打开。
look fixedly at something = 目不转睛地看着某物
⑤ Fixed costs stay put, fixed assets depreciate, and fixed-term contracts end.
= 固定成本不动，固定资产折旧，定期合同到期。
fixed costs = 固定成本
fixed assets = 固定资产
fixed-term = 定期的
⑥ A fixed-line phone survives the power cut that takes out everything else.
= 停电时别的全瘫了，固定电话还能用。
fixed-line = 固定线路的
⑦ Fixed-wing aircraft need a runway and helicopters need somewhere flat.
= 固定翼飞机要跑道，直升机只要一块平地。
fixed-wing = 固定翼的

fixer
核心：两支。一是（常含贬义）疏通门路的人、掮客；二是（摄影）定影液。可数。
① Every embassy has a fixer whose job title says something else entirely.
= 每个使馆都有个专办疏通的人，头衔上写的完全是别的。
a fixer = 疏通门路的人
② The fixer stops the image changing and the smell stays in the room for days.
= 定影液让影像不再变化，那股味道在屋里留好几天。
fixer = 定影液
③ The house is a fixer-upper and the survey on it runs to nine pages.
= 那栋房子是待整修的旧屋，验房报告写了九页。
a fixer-upper = 待整修的房子

fixings
核心：两支。一是（美式）配菜、佐料：turkey with all the fixings；
二是（英式）五金配件：螺丝、膨胀塞那一类。作复数。
① Turkey with all the fixings takes two ovens and one very long morning.
= 火鸡配齐全套配菜，要两个烤箱加一个漫长的上午。
all the fixings = 全套配菜
② The shelf comes flat and the fixings come in a bag that splits.
= 那个架子是平板包装，配件装在一个会破的袋子里。
fixings = 五金配件

fixity
核心：（正式）固定不变、执着不移：fixity of purpose 是意志坚定。不可数。
① Fixity of purpose got the bridge built and cost him every friendship.
= 意志的坚定让那座桥建成了，也让他失去了所有友谊。
fixity of purpose = 意志坚定
② The fixity of that stare made the interview a very short one.
= 那种一动不动的注视，让面谈变得极短。
the fixity of something = 某物的固定不变

fixture
核心：三支。一是（英式，体育）赛程、一场比赛：a home fixture；
二是固定装置：light fixtures；三是（口语）某地的老面孔、常驻人物。可数。
① The home fixture was moved twice and played in front of almost nobody.
= 那场主场比赛改了两次期，最后几乎没人看。
a home fixture = 主场比赛
② Light fixtures stay with the house and the lampshades leave with the tenant.
= 灯具随房子留下，灯罩跟着租客走。
light fixtures = 灯具固定装置
③ He is a fixture in that café and orders the same thing at the same hour.
= 他是那家咖啡馆的常驻人物，每天同一个钟点点同样的东西。
a fixture = 常驻的人或物

fizz
核心：两支。一是气泡与那阵嘶嘶声：lose its fizz；二是（非正式）起泡酒。
名词与动词同形。
① The lemonade lost its fizz in the sun and nobody drank the rest.
= 汽水在太阳底下跑了气，剩下的没人喝。
lose its fizz = 跑气
② A glass of fizz at eleven in the morning means somebody has retired.
= 上午十一点端出起泡酒，说明有人退休了。
fizz = 起泡酒

fizzer
核心：（澳新，非正式）哑炮、砸了的事：说本该热闹、结果没劲的场面。可数。
① The concert was a fizzer and the queue outside had been two hours long.
= 那场演出砸了，外面的队原本排了两个钟头。
a fizzer = 砸了的事、哑炮
② A campaign that starts loud and ends as a fizzer costs more than silence.
= 一场开头声势浩大、收尾成哑炮的宣传，比不吭声还贵。
turn out to be a fizzer = 结果落了空

fizzle
核心：（非正式）虎头蛇尾地收场：多说 fizzle out。
① The protest fizzled out in the rain and reconvened on a sunnier Tuesday.
= 那场抗议在雨里不了了之，换到一个晴些的周二重来。
fizzle out = 虎头蛇尾地结束
② Interest fizzled after the second meeting and the fund stayed unspent.
= 兴致在第二次会后就淡了，那笔钱一直没花出去。
fizzle = 渐渐消散

fizzy
核心：（英式）带气的、起泡的：fizzy drinks 就是汽水。
① Fizzy drinks are banned in that school and sold from a van outside it.
= 那所学校禁汽水，校门外一辆货车照卖。
fizzy drinks = 汽水
② The water is fizzy and the label says still in three languages.
= 那水是带气的，标签上却用三种语言写着无气。
fizzy = 带气的

flab
核心：（非正式，贬）赘肉：身上松垮的那层肥肉。不可数。形容词 flabby。
① Three months of that job put flab on everybody who took the lift.
= 那份工作干三个月，凡是坐电梯的都长了赘肉。
flab = 赘肉
② He talks about flab and orders exactly the same lunch every day.
= 他把赘肉挂在嘴上，午饭天天点同一样。
lose the flab = 减掉赘肉

flabbergasted
核心：（非正式）目瞪口呆的：吃惊得说不出话，多作表语。
① She was flabbergasted by the bill and paid it without a single word.
= 那张账单让她目瞪口呆，她一声不吭就付了。
be flabbergasted = 目瞪口呆
② The committee looked flabbergasted and voted for it anyway.
= 委员会一脸错愕，还是投了赞成票。
look flabbergasted = 一脸错愕

flabby
核心：两支。一是（肌肉）松弛的；二是（论证、文字）松散无力的。
① Flabby arms and a fast bowling arm rarely belong to the same person.
= 松垮的胳膊跟快投球的手臂，很少长在同一个人身上。
flabby muscles = 松弛的肌肉
② The middle of the essay is flabby and the last page just about saves it.
= 那篇文章中段松散无力，最后一页勉强把它救了回来。
flabby writing = 松散无力的文字

flaccid
核心：（正式）松软的、蔫的：说组织与植物，也说表演与市场没劲。
① The leaves go flaccid within an hour of being picked.
= 那些叶子摘下来不到一小时就蔫了。
flaccid = 松软的、蔫的
② A flaccid performance in the second half lost a match they had led.
= 下半场软绵绵的表现，把本来领先的比赛输掉了。
a flaccid performance = 软绵绵的表现

flack
核心：两支（多为美式）。一是（贬）公关、宣传人员；二是 flak 的另一种拼法。可数。
① The flack answered every question with the same three sentences.
= 那位公关拿同样三句话回答所有问题。
a flack = 公关（贬）
② Getting flack from two directions at once is normal in that job.
= 在那份工作里，两头同时挨骂是常事。
get flack = 挨骂

flag
核心：四支。一是旗：fly the flag、raise a flag；二是动词，标出、提请注意；
三是动词，（兴致、体力）减退；四是 flag down，招手拦车。
① They fly the flag on four days a year and take it in when it rains.
= 他们一年挂四天旗，下雨就收进来。
fly the flag = 挂旗、扬旗
raise a flag = 升旗
② She flagged the clause and nobody read the note she left beside it.
= 她把那一条标了出来，旁边留的批注没人看。
flag something = 把某物标出来
③ Interest flagged after the second speech and revived at the buffet.
= 第二段讲话之后兴致就淡了，到自助餐才回来。
flag = 减退、发蔫
④ He flagged down a taxi that already had somebody sitting in it.
= 他招手拦下一辆已经载了人的出租车。
flag down = 招手拦下

flagellate
核心：两支。一是（正式、宗教）鞭笞：把自我鞭打当苦修；
二是（生物）有鞭毛的：flagellate cells 靠甩动鞭毛移动。
① Some orders flagellate themselves at Easter and the practice is dying out.
= 有些修会在复活节自我鞭笞，这做法正在消失。
flagellate yourself = 自我鞭笞
② Flagellate cells move by whipping a single thread behind them.
= 有鞭毛的细胞靠甩动身后那根丝来移动。
flagellate cells = 鞭毛细胞

flagellation
核心：（正式）鞭笞：作为刑罚或苦修的抽打。不可数。
① Flagellation appears in the rule of that order and in none of its accounts.
= 鞭笞写在那个修会的会规里，账目里一次也没出现过。
flagellation = 鞭笞
② Self-flagellation over one mistake wastes the day that could have fixed it.
= 为一个错误自责不休，把本可以补救的那天白白浪费了。
self-flagellation = 自责不休

flageolet
核心：两支。一是（乐器）小竖笛；二是（法餐）小四季豆，青白色的小菜豆。可数。
① The flageolet is played like a recorder and sounds an octave higher.
= 小竖笛的吹法跟竖笛一样，音高一个八度。
a flageolet = 小竖笛
② Flageolet beans go with lamb and take an hour longer than the recipe says.
= 小四季豆配羊肉，比食谱上写的多炖一个钟头。
flageolet beans = 小四季豆

flagon
核心：（旧或正式）大酒壶、细颈大瓶：宴席上倒酒用的那种。可数。
① The flagon on that table holds two litres and pours like a watering can.
= 那张桌上的大酒壶装两升，倒起来像洒水壶。
a flagon = 大酒壶
② Two flagons of cider disappeared before the food had even arrived.
= 两壶苹果酒在上菜之前就没了。
a flagon of cider = 一壶苹果酒

flagpole
核心：旗杆：立在地上或屋顶的那根杆子。可数。
习语 run something up the flagpole 是把主意抛出去试反应。
① The flagpole was put up in 1953 and the rope has been replaced eleven times.
= 那根旗杆 1953 年立起来，绳子换过十一回。
a flagpole = 旗杆
② Run it up the flagpole and see whether anybody salutes.
= 把这主意抛出去，看有没有人买账。
run something up the flagpole = 抛出主意试探反应

flagrant
核心：（贬）明目张胆的、公然的：多修饰 breach、violation、disregard。副词 flagrantly。
① A flagrant breach of the rules went unpunished for three whole seasons.
= 一次明目张胆的违规，整整三个赛季都没受罚。
a flagrant breach = 公然的违规
② The flagrant disregard for that sign is why it was replaced with a gate.
= 正因为对那块牌子公然不理，它后来换成了一道门。
flagrant disregard = 公然的无视
副词 flagrantly：明目张胆地，多修饰 ignored、breached、violated。
③ He flagrantly ignored the queue and nobody in it said a word.
= 他明目张胆地插队，排队的人没一个吭声。
flagrantly ignore something = 公然无视某事
④ The terms were flagrantly breached and the contract ran another year.
= 那些条款被公然违反，合同还是又执行了一年。
be flagrantly breached = 被公然违反

flagship
核心：两支。一是（海军）旗舰；二是引申的招牌项目或旗舰店。可数。
① The flagship store opened in March and closed the following January.
= 那家旗舰店三月开业，第二年一月就关了门。
a flagship store = 旗舰店
② The flagship carries the admiral and the smallest galley in the fleet.
= 旗舰上载着海军上将，还有全舰队最小的厨房。
a flagship = 旗舰

flagstaff
核心：旗杆：跟 flagpole 同义，略正式，多见于官方与航海语境。可数。
① The flagstaff on the fort is taller than anything else on the island.
= 堡垒上那根旗杆，比岛上任何东西都高。
a flagstaff = 旗杆
② A new flagstaff was shipped in and the old one became a gatepost.
= 新旗杆运了过来，旧的那根当了门柱。

flagstone
核心：石板：铺路铺地用的大块扁平石头。可数。
① The flagstones are uneven and the council has measured them twice.
= 那些石板高低不平，市政厅量过两回。
a flagstone = 石板
② A flagstone floor stays cold in August and warm in nobody's memory.
= 石板地面八月里还是凉的，谁也想不起它什么时候暖过。
a flagstone floor = 石板地面

flail
核心：三支。一是乱挥乱舞：flail your arms；二是名词，连枷，打谷的旧农具；
三是引申，手忙脚乱地应付。
① He went in with his arms flailing and came out with a mouthful of water.
= 他胳膊乱挥着下了水，上来时呛了一口。
flail your arms = 乱挥手臂
② The department is flailing and the report calls it a transition.
= 那个部门正手忙脚乱，报告里管这叫过渡期。
flail = 手忙脚乱

flair
核心：两支。一是天赋、擅长：have a flair for something；
二是做事的派头与风格：do something with flair。不可数。
① She has a flair for languages and no patience for grammar drills.
= 她学语言有天赋，做语法练习却没耐心。
have a flair for something = 在某方面有天赋
② The whole thing was done with flair and finished eleven days late.
= 整件事办得很有派头，晚了十一天才完成。
with flair = 有派头地

flak
核心：两支。一是高射炮火：a flak jacket 是防弹背心；
二是（非正式）猛烈批评：take flak、get flak。不可数。
① He took a lot of flak for a decision that had been made above him.
= 一个上头做的决定，让他挨了不少批评。
take flak = 挨批评
② A flak jacket weighs nine kilos and stops what it is designed to stop.
= 一件防弹背心重九公斤，该挡的它挡得住。
a flak jacket = 防弹背心

flake
核心：三支。一是薄片：flakes of paint；二是动词，剥落：flake off；
三是（美式，非正式）不靠谱的人。可数。形容词 flaky。
① Flakes of paint came off the ceiling and landed in the soup.
= 天花板上的漆片剥落下来，掉进了汤里。
a flake = 薄片
② The paint flakes off the moment that room gets damp.
= 那屋里一潮，漆就剥落。
flake off = 剥落
③ He is a flake and everybody plans around him rather than with him.
= 他不靠谱，人人都绕开他安排，而不是跟他一起安排。
a flake = 不靠谱的人

flakiness
核心：两支。一是酥脆易碎，说酥皮；二是（非正式）不靠谱。不可数。
① The flakiness of that pastry depends on how cold the butter was.
= 那种酥皮酥不酥，全看黄油有多凉。
the flakiness of something = 某物的酥脆
② His flakiness costs the team a player at every away game.
= 他的不靠谱，让球队每次客场都少一个人。
flakiness = 不靠谱

flaky
核心：三支。一是酥脆的：flaky pastry；二是剥落的：flaky paint；
三是（非正式）不靠谱的、古怪的。
① Flaky pastry needs cold hands and an even colder kitchen.
= 做酥皮要手凉，厨房更要凉。
flaky pastry = 酥皮
② The flaky paint on that door has been photographed by three art students.
= 那扇门上剥落的漆，被三个艺术生拍过。
flaky paint = 剥落的漆
③ A flaky supplier is worse to work with than an expensive one.
= 跟一个不靠谱的供应商打交道，比跟一个贵的更糟。
flaky = 不靠谱的

flambé
核心：（烹饪）淋酒点火烹制的：也作动词。
① The pancakes are served flambé and the smoke alarm has learned to expect it.
= 那些薄饼是燃着酒端上来的，烟雾报警器已经习惯了。
flambé = 燃酒烹制的
② He flambéed it at the table and singed one of his eyebrows.
= 他在桌边点了火，燎掉了半边眉毛。
flambé something = 给某物淋酒点火

flamboyance
核心：张扬、华丽：衣着、举止与风格上的招摇。不可数。
① The flamboyance of the costumes distracted from a very thin plot.
= 戏服的张扬，掩住了单薄得可怜的情节。
flamboyance = 张扬华丽
② Flamboyance in that courtroom is a tactic and not a temperament.
= 在那间法庭上，张扬是一种策略，不是性情。
with flamboyance = 招摇地

flamboyant
核心：张扬的、招摇的：说人、衣着与风格夺目而不含蓄。副词 flamboyantly。
① A flamboyant coat and a very quiet voice is an effective combination.
= 一件招摇的大衣配一副极轻的嗓音，是个很有效的组合。
a flamboyant coat = 招摇的大衣
② His flamboyant style filled the gallery and emptied the budget.
= 他那种张扬的风格填满了画廊，也花光了预算。
a flamboyant style = 张扬的风格
副词 flamboyantly：张扬地、招摇地，多修饰 dressed、gestured、decorated。
③ She dresses flamboyantly and answers questions in three words.
= 她穿得招摇，回答问题只用三个词。
dress flamboyantly = 穿得张扬
④ The hall is flamboyantly decorated and completely impossible to heat.
= 那个大厅装饰得极尽华丽，却根本没法供暖。
be flamboyantly decorated = 装饰得极尽华丽

flame
核心：三支。一是火焰：burst into flames、go up in flames；
二是旧情人：an old flame；三是（网络）骂帖，也作动词。
① The shed burst into flames and the fire brigade blamed the wiring.
= 那间棚子腾地烧了起来，消防队怪线路。
burst into flames = 突然着火
go up in flames = 付之一炬
② An old flame turned up at the funeral and stood right at the back.
= 一位旧情人来了葬礼，站在最后头。
an old flame = 旧情人
③ He flamed a stranger online and apologised by private message.
= 他在网上骂了个陌生人，又私信道歉。
flame somebody = 在网上骂某人

flamenco
核心：弗拉门戈：西班牙南部的歌舞与吉他音乐，节奏靠拍手与踏步打出来。不可数。
① Flamenco is counted in twelves and nobody in the audience hears where it starts.
= 弗拉门戈按十二拍数，观众里没人听得出它从哪儿起。
flamenco = 弗拉门戈
② A flamenco guitar is lighter than a classical one and struck rather than plucked.
= 弗拉门戈吉他比古典吉他轻，是扫弦而不是拨弦。
a flamenco guitar = 弗拉门戈吉他

flameproof
核心：耐火的、防火的：说材料与器皿，能上明火而不坏。
① The dish is flameproof and the lid is not, which nobody had read.
= 那只碗能上明火，盖子不能 —— 说明谁都没看说明。
flameproof = 耐火的
② Flameproof overalls are checked twice a year and worn every single day.
= 防火工作服一年检查两次，天天都穿。
flameproof overalls = 防火工作服

flamethrower
核心：喷火器：喷出燃烧液体的武器，也用来烧荒除草。可数。
① A flamethrower clears a firebreak faster than nine people with spades.
= 一台喷火器开出防火带，比九个人拿铁锹还快。
a flamethrower = 喷火器
fire a flamethrower = 喷射火焰
② The museum keeps a flamethrower behind glass and a warning beside it.
= 博物馆把一具喷火器摆在玻璃后头，旁边配了警示牌。

flaming
核心：三支。一是燃烧着的：a flaming torch；二是（颜色）火红的：flaming red；
三是（英式，非正式）表示恼火的加强词。
① A flaming torch at the front of the procession sets the pace.
= 队伍最前面一支燃着的火把，定了整队的步速。
a flaming torch = 燃着的火把
② Flaming red hair runs in that family and skips every other generation.
= 那家人的火红头发是遗传的，隔一代出一个。
flaming red = 火红的
③ The flaming thing has jammed again and the manual is out in the van.
= 那个鬼东西又卡住了，说明书还在车上。
a flaming nuisance = 讨厌透顶的麻烦

flamingo
核心：火烈鸟：腿长颈长、羽色粉红的水鸟。可数，复数 flamingos 或 flamingoes。
① A flamingo stands on one leg because the other one is warmer tucked up.
= 火烈鸟单腿站着，因为另一条腿收起来更暖和。
a flamingo = 火烈鸟
② The colour comes from what a flamingo eats and fades in captivity.
= 火烈鸟的颜色来自它吃的东西，圈养久了会褪。
a flock of flamingos = 一群火烈鸟

flammable
核心：易燃的：跟 inflammable 同义 —— 这一点最容易弄反；反义是 non-flammable。
① The label says flammable and the shelf above it holds the matches.
= 标签上写着易燃，上头那层架子放着火柴。
flammable = 易燃的
② Highly flammable liquids go in the metal cabinet by the door.
= 高度易燃的液体，放在门边那个铁柜里。
highly flammable = 高度易燃的

flan
核心：两支。一是（英式）果馅挞、咸味挞：底子是酥皮或蛋糕坯；
二是（西班牙与拉美）焦糖蛋奶冻。可数。
① The flan came out of the tin in one piece and went back in three.
= 那个挞完整地脱了模，装回去时成了三块。
a flan = 果馅挞
② In Spain a 「flan」 is a set custard and has nothing to do with pastry.
= 在西班牙，「flan」是凝住的蛋奶冻，跟酥皮毫无关系。
a caramel flan = 焦糖蛋奶冻

flange
核心：（机械）法兰、凸缘：管子或轮子边上突出的一圈边，用来连接或卡住。可数。
① The flange bolts to the next section and the gasket goes between them.
= 那个法兰用螺栓接到下一段，垫片夹在中间。
a flange = 法兰
② A train wheel has a flange on the inside and stays on the rail because of it.
= 火车轮内侧有一圈轮缘，全靠它才不脱轨。
a wheel flange = 轮缘

flank
核心：三支。一是（人或动物的）胁部；二是（军事）侧翼：attack the flank；
三是动词，位于两侧：be flanked by something。
① The flank of the horse steams in cold air and the rider never notices.
= 天冷时马的胁部冒着热气，骑手从没留意过。
the flank = 胁部
② An attack on the flank ended a battle that the centre could not win.
= 一次侧翼进攻，终结了中路打不赢的那场仗。
attack the flank = 攻击侧翼
③ The gate is flanked by two trees that are older than the wall itself.
= 那道门两侧各有一棵树，比墙本身还老。
be flanked by something = 两侧有某物

flanker
核心：（橄榄球）侧翼前锋：负责争抢与擒抱的边位球员。可数。
① The flanker gets to every breakdown and gets none of the credit.
= 那位侧翼前锋每个争球点都到，功劳一点没有。
a flanker = 侧翼前锋
an open-side flanker = 开阔侧的侧翼前锋
② Two flankers and a number eight do most of the tackling.
= 两名侧翼前锋加一名八号，包了大半的擒抱。

flannel
核心：三支。一是法兰绒：起绒的软毛料；二是（英式）洗脸巾；
三是（英式，非正式）含糊其辞的搪塞。
① A flannel shirt outlives three coats and looks worse every winter.
= 一件法兰绒衬衫比三件外套还耐穿，一年比一年难看。
a flannel shirt = 法兰绒衬衫
② The answer was pure flannel and the question got asked all over again.
= 那个回答纯属搪塞，问题又被从头问了一遍。
flannel = 含糊其辞的搪塞

flannelette
核心：棉法兰绒：起绒的棉布，做床单与睡衣用。不可数。
① Flannelette sheets are warm in January and unbearable by March.
= 棉绒床单一月里暖和，到三月就受不了。
flannelette sheets = 棉绒床单
② Flannelette catches fire a good deal faster than anybody expects.
= 棉法兰绒着起火来，比谁想的都快得多。
flannelette = 棉法兰绒

flap
核心：四支。一是（翅膀、旗子）拍动：flap its wings；二是（信封、口袋的）翻盖；
三是（飞机的）襟翼；四是（英式，非正式）慌乱：be in a flap。
① The bird flapped its wings twice and got nowhere at all.
= 那只鸟拍了两下翅膀，一点没飞起来。
flap its wings = 拍打翅膀
② The flap on the envelope was licked, sealed and torn open by the wrong person.
= 信封的封口舔过、封好，却被不该拆的人撕开了。
a flap = 封口、翻盖
③ She was in a flap about a train that turned out to be delayed anyway.
= 她为一班车慌得不行，那班车最后反正晚点了。
be in a flap = 慌成一团

flapjack
核心：两支。一是（英式）燕麦糖块：燕麦加黄油糖浆烤成的方块；
二是（美式）薄煎饼。可数。
① A flapjack is mostly butter and entirely necessary on a long walk.
= 燕麦糖块基本上是黄油做的，长途步行少不了它。
a flapjack = 燕麦糖块
② Flapjacks in America arrive in a stack with syrup poured over the top.
= 在美国，薄煎饼是一摞端上来，上面浇着糖浆。
a flapjack = 薄煎饼（美式）

flapper
核心：（历史）二十年代的摩登女子：短发、短裙、跳舞抽烟的那一代。可数。
① A flapper in that photograph is holding a cigarette and somebody else's hat.
= 照片里那位摩登女郎，手里拿着烟，还有别人的帽子。
a flapper = 二十年代的摩登女子
the flapper era = 摩登女郎的年代
② Flappers shocked their mothers and bored their daughters.
= 摩登女郎吓着了母亲那一辈，又让女儿那一辈嫌闷。

flare
核心：四支。一是（火）突然蹿旺：flare up；二是（脾气、病情）突然发作：tempers flare；
三是名词，信号弹、照明弹；四是（裤腿、裙摆）张开。形容词 flared。
① The fire flared up when the door opened and died down again.
= 门一开火苗蹿了起来，随后又落下去。
flare up = 突然蹿旺、突然发作
② Tempers flared over a parking space and cooled over a cup of tea.
= 为一个车位火气蹿上来，一杯茶下去又消了。
tempers flare = 火气上来
③ A flare was fired at two in the morning and answered nine minutes later.
= 凌晨两点打了一发信号弹，九分钟后有了回应。
a flare = 信号弹

flared
核心：（裤腿、裙摆）喇叭状张开的：flared trousers 就是喇叭裤。
① Flared trousers came back into fashion and that shop had kept nine pairs.
= 喇叭裤又流行起来，那家店一直留着九条。
flared trousers = 喇叭裤
② A flared skirt hides the chair you are sitting on.
= 喇叭裙连你坐的椅子都能遮住。
a flared skirt = 喇叭裙

flarepath
核心：（航空）跑道灯道：夜间用灯标出来的着陆通道。可数。
① The flarepath was lit by hand and the aircraft came in on the third pass.
= 那条灯道是人工点亮的，飞机盘旋到第三圈才落下来。
a flarepath = 跑道灯道
light the flarepath = 点亮跑道灯道
② Two lamps on the flarepath had gone out and nobody had said so.
= 灯道上两盏灯灭了，谁也没说。

flash
核心：五支。一是闪光：a flash of light；二是（相机）闪光灯；
三是动词，一闪、亮出：flash a badge；四是（英式，贬）花哨的；
五是习语：in a flash 是一转眼，a flash in the pan 是昙花一现。
① A flash of light behind the hill turned out to be a car on the bend.
= 山后一道闪光，原来是弯道上的一辆车。
a flash of light = 一道闪光
② The flash fired and the photograph came out darker than the room.
= 闪光灯闪了，照片却比屋里还暗。
a flash = 闪光灯
③ He flashed a badge at the door and nobody actually looked at it.
= 他在门口亮了下证件，其实没人细看。
flash a badge = 亮一下证件
④ It was over in a flash and the queue outside had not moved.
= 一转眼就完事了，外面的队一步没动。
in a flash = 一转眼
⑤ That success was a flash in the pan and the second album says so.
= 那次成功不过昙花一现，第二张专辑说明了这一点。
a flash in the pan = 昙花一现

flashback
核心：两支。一是（影视、小说）闪回：插进去的过去片段；
二是（心理）创伤闪回：突然重历过去的场景。可数。
① The flashback explains the first ten minutes and ruins the surprise.
= 那段闪回解释了开头十分钟，也毁掉了那个悬念。
a flashback = 闪回
② Flashbacks come without warning and go when somebody says a name.
= 创伤闪回毫无预兆地来，有人叫一声名字又走了。
have a flashback = 出现闪回

flashbulb
核心：（旧）闪光灯泡：一次性的摄影闪光灯。可数。
① A flashbulb went off and left a green square in everybody's eyes.
= 闪光灯泡一亮，人人眼里都留下一块绿方块。
a flashbulb = 闪光灯泡
a spent flashbulb = 用过的闪光灯泡
② Photographers carried flashbulbs by the boxful and threw them away hot.
= 摄影师整盒整盒地带着闪光灯泡，用完趁热就扔。

flasher
核心：两支。一是（贬）暴露狂：在公共场合露体的人；二是车辆的闪光信号灯。可数。
① A flasher in the park was arrested on a Tuesday and named on the Friday.
= 公园里一名暴露狂周二被捕，周五被公布姓名。
a flasher = 暴露狂
② The hazard flasher stays on and the battery is flat by morning.
= 双闪一直亮着，到早上电瓶就没电了。
a flasher = 闪光信号灯

flashgun
核心：（摄影）闪光灯组件：装在相机上的那个独立闪光装置。可数。
① The flashgun sits on top of the camera and doubles its weight.
= 那个闪光灯装在相机顶上，重量翻了一倍。
a flashgun = 闪光灯组件
fit a flashgun = 装上闪光灯
② A flashgun on a cable can be held anywhere in the room.
= 用连线的闪光灯，举在屋里哪个位置都行。

flask
核心：三支。一是保温瓶：a vacuum flask；二是（实验室）烧瓶：a conical flask；
三是随身扁酒壶：a hip flask。可数。
① The flask keeps tea hot for nine hours and tastes of every soup before it.
= 那个保温瓶能保温九个钟头，也带着此前每一种汤的味道。
a vacuum flask = 保温瓶
② A conical flask is easier to swirl and harder to knock over.
= 锥形烧瓶更好摇，也更不容易碰倒。
a conical flask = 锥形烧瓶
③ He carries a hip flask to weddings and offers it to nobody.
= 他去婚礼带着随身扁酒壶，不给任何人喝。
a hip flask = 随身扁酒壶

flat
核心：五支。一是平的：a flat surface；二是（英式）公寓：rent a flat；
三是（轮胎）没气的：a flat tyre；四是（饮料）跑了气的；
五是习语：flat out 是拼尽全力，flat broke 是身无分文。副词 flatly。
① The floor is not flat and every table in the room rocks.
= 那地面不平，屋里每张桌子都晃。
a flat surface = 平面
② They rent a flat above a bakery and get up when the ovens do.
= 他们租了面包房楼上的公寓，烤箱几点开工他们几点起。
rent a flat = 租一套公寓
③ A flat tyre on that road means a nine-mile walk.
= 在那条路上爆胎，就意味着走九英里。
a flat tyre = 没气的轮胎
④ The lemonade went flat and the party went on regardless.
= 汽水跑了气，派对照旧。
go flat = 跑气
⑤ She worked flat out for a fortnight and then slept for two days.
= 她拼了两个星期，然后睡了两天。
flat out = 拼尽全力
⑥ He was flat broke in March and buying a boat by August.
= 他三月身无分文，到八月已经在看船了。
flat broke = 身无分文
副词 flatly：一是断然地：flatly refused；二是（语气）平板无起伏地。
⑦ She flatly refused and gave a reason nobody had thought of.
= 她断然拒绝，给的理由谁也没想到。
flatly refuse = 断然拒绝
⑧ He read the good news flatly and the bad news with feeling.
= 好消息他念得毫无起伏，坏消息倒念得动情。
read something flatly = 平板地念出

flatbed
核心：两支。一是平板货车：车斗是敞开的平板；二是平板扫描仪。可数。
① The flatbed took the beams and the crane took the rest of the morning.
= 平板货车拉走了大梁，吊车占掉了上午剩下的时间。
a flatbed = 平板货车
